### 未纳入当前实现清单的判定口径

| 项目 | 说明 |
| --- | --- |
| 范围 | 本文件独立存放当前不能直接按现有字段和闭合契约实现的原条目；同时单独记录重复映射。未核定不是已证明闭源，也不是永远不可实现。只缺周期/种子、拆列、旧API等可修复原因已按本地固定口径恢复到主报告；本文件仍存的过程性原因明确标为待验证，不能假称字段缺失。 |
| 数量 | 原始来源ID共3117个；未纳入原条目1397个，数学重复映射114条，另外列出模板内未恢复的单列输出。一个来源ID可能拆为多列，因此两文件行数不能直接相加冒充原始因子数。 |
| 主报告 | [可实现报告](公开因子库_最新版字段支持报告.md)；本文件的别名行不另生成因子文件。 |

### 原因类别与严重性

| 类别 | 严重性 | 含义与恢复条件 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C1 必需数据或经济科目缺口 | 高 | 补充必需原始字段/市场历史或明确同义科目后才可恢复；不得用看似相近科目替代。 |
| C2 外部公共序列或组合接口 | 高 | 缺完整市场/无风险/因子收益等输入，或原目标为公共多空收益而不是逐股信号；须另建数据/组合模块。 |
| C3 未来数据依赖 | 高 | 原输出需要未来价格或确认值，不能原样用于当时选股；如改确认日或因果定义须作为明确新版本重新验收。 |
| C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义 | 中至高 | 缺核心运算、财务范围或相互矛盾的定义，补一个窗口不能保证同因子；须补一级来源证据或另立明确版本。 |
| C5 常量或辅助接口 | 高（作为独立因子） | 恒定归一项、参数分发/索引/任意外部序列处理器不构成固定逐股经济因子；可作为实现工具。 |
| C6 实现契约尚待验证 | 中；若仅工程参数则低 | 公开算法并未被证明不可实现，但尚无已验证的字段、窗口、初始化、输出及边界闭合契约；继续源级展开后可恢复，不能称闭源。 |
| D 数学重复 | 无需恢复 | 可以计算，因同一完整数学契约已由主报告代表项实现而不重复收录；按映射查代表项。 |

| MAVP.outReal（Minimum Period=2, Maximum Period=30, MA Type=0）；PF00810 | 源码核心递推表达式（完整分支/参数见链接）：outputSize = endIdx - firstOut + 1;；for( i = 0; i &lt; outputSize; i += 1 )；tempPeriod = inPeriods[startIdx + i];；for( curPeriod = minUsed; curPeriod &lt;= maxUsed + 1; curPeriod += 1 )；for( curPeriod = minUsed; curPeriod &lt;= maxUsed; curPeriod += 1 )；retCode = TA_MA(startIdx,startIdx + lastOccurrence,inReal,curPeriod,optInMAType,&amp;localBegIdx,&amp;localNbElement,localOutputArray);；for( i = bucketStart; i &lt; bucketEnd; i += 1 )；tempPeriod = (double)inPeriods[startIdx + i];；retCode = TA_S_MA(startIdx,startIdx + lastOccurrence,inReal,curPeriod,optInMAType,&amp;localBegIdx,&amp;localNbElement,localOutputArray);；sp-&gt;tape = (double *)TA_Malloc( sizeof(double) * (size_t)size );；for( b = historyLen &gt; size ? historyLen - size : 0; b &lt; historyLen; b++ )；double *win = localWin;；win = (double *)TA_Malloc( sizeof(double) * (size_t)(lb + 1) );；for( i = 0; i &lt; lb; i++ )；本行取输出outReal。[算法源码](https://github.com/TA-Lib/ta-lib/blob/481dda1b10350468e82221b17fe84f6bb24e122d/src/ta_func/ta_MAVP.c)；全部来源：[TA-Lib · MAVP.outReal（Minimum Period=2, Maximum Period=30, MA Type=0）](https://github.com/TA-Lib/ta-lib/blob/481dda1b10350468e82221b17fe84f6bb24e122d/src/ta_func/ta_MAVP.c) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；periods（研究参数序列，文档未列） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；该公开实现的完整状态/默认参数/输入或单一输出未在本轮独立数值契约中核定；属于待核定，不认定为闭源或数学上不可能实现 |

| WILLIAMS_FRACTAL（period=2）；PF01034 | 源码算子/递推链：window_size = period * 2 + 1；bearish_fractals = Series(ohlc.high.rolling(window=window_size, center=True).apply(is_bearish_fractal, raw=True), name='BearishFractal')；bullish_fractals = Series(ohlc.low.rolling(window=window_size, center=True).apply(is_bullish_fractal, raw=True), name='BullishFractal')；输出=concat([bearish_fractals, bullish_fractals], axis=1)；输出=False；输出=True；确切输出列/初始化见[源码](https://github.com/peerchemist/finta/blob/af01fa594995de78f5ada5c336e61cd87c46b151/finta/finta.py#L2208)。；全部来源：[FinTA · WILLIAMS_FRACTAL（period=2）](https://github.com/peerchemist/finta/blob/af01fa594995de78f5ada5c336e61cd87c46b151/finta/finta.py#L2208) | `open_hfq`（`stock_day`）；`high_hfq`（`stock_day`）；`low_hfq`（`stock_day`）；`close_hfq`（`stock_day`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；旧目录此项按多列指标方法计数，尚未逐列核定单因子输出与参数；不把整个DataFrame当一个BaseFactor信号 |

| eq_dur（Equity duration）；PF01742 | ROE0=ni_x/be_x[-1年]，g0=sale_x/sale_x[-1年]−1；ROE_j=0.12(1−0.57)+0.57ROE_{j−1}，g_j=0.06(1−0.24)+0.24g_{j−1}；B_j=B_{j−1}(1+g_j)，CD_j=B_{j−1}(ROE_j−g_j)，B0=be_x；D=Σ_{j=1…10}jCD_j/(1.12)^j/MC+(10+1.12/0.12)(1−ΣCD_j/(1.12)^j/MC)；原源码美元换算fx、有效性检查照原文；[原算法](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors)；全部来源：[JKP · eq_dur（Equity duration）](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors) | ceq→`total_hldr_eqy_exc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；txditc→`defer_tax_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；sale→`revenue`（`financial_income`）；ib→`n_income`（`financial_income`）；`close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；`total_mv`（`stock_day`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；缺失：优先股账面价值 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：不支持原定义（缺上述输入）；已有字段只能形成另行命名的简化/适配版本 |

| f_score（Pitroski F-score）；PF01791 | 原JKP宏定义较复杂；下列为PyAnomaly对应的公开实现算子链，非已证明数值等价：at_1 = lag(at_, 1)；f_roa = ib / at_1；f_croa = ocf / at_1；f_droa = diff(f_roa)；f_acc = f_croa - f_roa；f_liq = diff(act / lct)；f_gm = diff(gp / sale)；f_aturn = diff(sale / at_1)；if config.replicate_jkp:<br>    f_lev = diff(dltt / at_)<br>    f_eqis = sstk<br>else:<br>    f_lev = diff(dltt / avgat)<br>    f_eqis = (sstk - pstk).fillna(0)；char = (f_roa &gt; ZERO).astype(int) + (f_croa &gt; ZERO).astype(int) + (f_droa &gt; ZERO).astype(int) + (f_acc &gt; ZERO).astype(int) + (f_lev &lt; ZERO).astype(int) + (f_liq &gt; ZERO).astype(int) + is_zero(f_eqis).astype(int) + (f_gm &gt; ZERO).astype(int) + (f_aturn &gt; ZERO).astype(int)；输出=char.astype(config.float_type)；[原算法](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors)；全部来源：[JKP · f_score（Pitroski F-score）](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors) | ROA→`roa`（`financial_indicator`）；at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；act→`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lct→`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dltt→`lt_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；sale→`revenue`（`financial_income`）；cogs→`oper_cost`（`financial_income`）；ib→`n_income`（`financial_income`）；oancf→`n_cashflow_act`（`financial_cash_flow`）；sstk→`c_recp_cap_contrib`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_indicator`）；`end_date`（`financial_indicator`）；缺失：优先股账面价值 | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：待核实（原JKP宏公式尚未完整展开；对应实现仅作参考）；不支持原定义（缺上述输入）；已有字段只能形成另行命名的简化/适配版本 |
| o_score（Ohlson O-score）；PF01792 | 原JKP宏定义较复杂；下列为PyAnomaly对应的公开实现算子链，非已证明数值等价：at = at_.copy()；nix = nix；nix_1 = lag(nix)；o_lev = debt / at；o_wc = (act - lct) / at；o_roe = nix / at；o_cacl = lct / act；o_cacl[act &lt; ZERO] = nan；o_ffo = (pi + dp) / lt_；o_ffo[lt_ &lt; ZERO] = nan；o_neg_eq = lt_ &gt; at_；o_neg_earn = (nix &lt; 0) &amp; (nix_1 &lt; 0)；denom = abs(nix) + abs(nix_1)；o_nich = (nix - nix_1) / denom；o_nich[denom &lt; ZERO] = nan；char = -1.32 - 0.407 * o_lat + 6.03 * o_lev + 1.43 * o_wc + 0.076 * o_cacl - 1.72 * o_neg_eq - 2.37 * o_roe - 1.83 * o_ffo + 0.285 * o_neg_earn - 0.52 * o_nich；输出=char；[原算法](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors)；全部来源：[JKP · o_score（Ohlson O-score）](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；act→`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lt→`total_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lct→`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dltt→`lt_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dlc→`st_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dp→`depr_fa_coga_dpba`（`financial_cash_flow`）；pi→`total_profit`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；缺失：优先股账面价值 | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：待核实（原JKP宏公式尚未完整展开；对应实现仅作参考）；不支持原定义（缺上述输入）；已有字段只能形成另行命名的简化/适配版本 |
| z_score（Altman Z-score）；PF01793 | 原JKP宏定义较复杂；下列为PyAnomaly对应的公开实现算子链，非已证明数值等价：z_wc = (act - lct) / at_；z_re = re / at_；z_eb = ebitda / at_；z_sa = sale / at_；z_me = me_fiscal / lt_；z_me[lt_ &lt;= 0] = nan；char = 1.2 * z_wc + 1.4 * z_re + 3.3 * z_eb + 0.6 * z_me + 1.0 * z_sa；输出=char；[原算法](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors)；全部来源：[JKP · z_score（Altman Z-score）](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；act→`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lt→`total_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lct→`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；sale→`revenue`（`financial_income`）；ebitda→`ebitda`（`financial_income`）；re→`retained_earnings`（`financial_indicator`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_indicator`）；`end_date`（`financial_indicator`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：待核实（原JKP宏公式尚未完整展开；对应实现仅作参考）；条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配） |
| pi_nix（Taxable income-to-book income）；PF01794 | pi_nix=pi_x / nix_x；pi_x=coalesce(pi, ebit_x-xint+coalesce(spi,0)+coalesce(nopi,0))；nix_x=coalesce(ni, ni_x+coalesce(xido_x, 0), ni_x + xi + do)；ebit_x=coalesce(ebit, oiadp, ebitda_x-dp)；ni_x=coalesce(ib, ni-xido_x, pi_x - txt - coalesce(mii, 0))；xido_x=coalesce(xido, xi+coalesce(do, 0))；[原算法](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors)；全部来源：[JKP · pi_nix（Taxable income-to-book income）](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors) | revt→`total_revenue`（`financial_income`）；cogs→`oper_cost`（`financial_income`）；ni→`n_income`（`financial_income`）；xsga→`admin_exp`（`financial_income`）；`sell_exp`（`financial_income`）；txt→`income_tax`（`financial_income`）；xint→`fin_exp_int_exp`（`financial_income`）；ebitda→`ebitda`（`financial_income`）；dp→`depr_fa_coga_dpba`（`financial_cash_flow`）；pi→`total_profit`（`financial_income`）；oiadp→`operate_profit`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`） | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；原非常项目前利润不等同中国净利润；原SG&amp;A需与sell_exp+admin_exp及研发归属核对 |
| at_be（Book leverage）；PF01795 | at_be=at_x / be_x；at_x=coalesce(at, seq_x + dltt + coalesce(lct, 0) + coalesce(lo, 0) + coalesce(txditc, 0))；be_x=seq_x+coalesce(txditc_x, 0)-coalesce(pstk_x, 0)；seq_x=coalesce(seq, ceq+coalesce(pstk_x, 0), at-lt)；txditc_x=coalesce(txditc, sum(txdb, itcb))；pstk_x=coalesce(pstkrv, pstkl, pstk)；[原算法](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors)；全部来源：[JKP · at_be（Book leverage）](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lt→`total_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lct→`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ceq→`total_hldr_eqy_exc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dltt→`lt_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lo→`oth_ncl`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；txditc→`defer_tax_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；缺失：优先股账面价值、优先股赎回价值、优先股清算价值；缺失：成立/上市/首次覆盖日期，最新版未列；不能用样本首日代替 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：不支持原定义（缺上述输入）；可另行定义简化版本 |

| kz_index（Kaplan-Zingales index）；PF01809 | 原JKP宏定义较复杂；下列为PyAnomaly对应的公开实现算子链，非已证明数值等价：ppent_1 = lag(ppent)；ppent_1[ppent_1 &lt;= 0] = nan；kz_cf = (ib + dp) / ppent_1；if config.replicate_jkp:<br>    kz_q = (at_ + me_fiscal - be) / at_<br>else:<br>    kz_q = (at_ + me_fiscal - be - txdb) / at_；kz_db = debt / (debt + seq)；kz_db[debt + seq == 0] = nan；kz_dv = dvt / ppent_1；kz_cs = che / ppent_1；char = -1.002 * kz_cf + 0.283 * kz_q + 3.139 * kz_db - 39.368 * kz_dv - 1.315 * kz_cs；输出=char；[原算法](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors)；全部来源：[JKP · kz_index（Kaplan-Zingales index）](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ceq→`total_hldr_eqy_exc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；che→`money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ppent→`fix_assets_total`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dltt→`lt_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dlc→`st_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；txditc→`defer_tax_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ib→`n_income`（`financial_income`）；dp→`depr_fa_coga_dpba`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；缺失：优先股账面价值、总股息原口径 | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：待核实（原JKP宏公式尚未完整展开；对应实现仅作参考）；不支持原定义（缺上述输入）；已有字段只能形成另行命名的简化/适配版本 |
| ocfq_saleq_std（Cash flow volatility）；PF01810 | 原JKP宏定义较复杂；下列为PyAnomaly对应的公开实现算子链，非已证明数值等价：if config.replicate_jkp:<br>    oancfq = oancfq.fillna(ibq + dpq)<br>else:<br>    oancfq = oancfq.fillna(ibq + dpq - diff(wcapq.fillna(0)))；char = rolling(oancfq / saleq, 16, 'std', min_n=8)；输出=char；[原算法](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors)；全部来源：[JKP · ocfq_saleq_std（Cash flow volatility）](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors) | sale→`revenue`（`financial_income`）；ib→`n_income`（`financial_income`）；oancf→`n_cashflow_act`（`financial_cash_flow`）；dp→`depr_fa_coga_dpba`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：待核实（原JKP宏公式尚未完整展开；对应实现仅作参考）；条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；原非常项目前利润不等同中国净利润 |
| cash_at（Cash-to-assets）；PF01811 | cash_at=che / at_x；at_x=coalesce(at, seq_x + dltt + coalesce(lct, 0) + coalesce(lo, 0) + coalesce(txditc, 0))；seq_x=coalesce(seq, ceq+coalesce(pstk_x, 0), at-lt)；[原算法](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors)；全部来源：[JKP · cash_at（Cash-to-assets）](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lt→`total_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lct→`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ceq→`total_hldr_eqy_exc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；che→`money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dltt→`lt_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lo→`oth_ncl`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；txditc→`defer_tax_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`） | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；需扣其他权益工具以确定普通股权益；原定义含短期投资，不只有money_cap；原长期债务范围比lt_borr宽，需加债券等；递延税与投资税抵免口径需核实 |
| ni_ar1（Earnings persistence）；PF01812 | 原JKP宏定义较复杂；下列为PyAnomaly对应的公开实现算子链，非已证明数值等价：if created('ni_ar1'):<br>    return；n = 5；reg = DataFrame(index=index)；reg['ni_at'] = ib / at_；reg['ni_at_lag1'] = lag(reg.ni_at)；res = rolling_regression(reg, n)；fa['ni_ar1'] = res[:, 1]；fa['ni_ivol'] = res[:, -1]；输出=data['ni_ar1']；[原算法](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors)；全部来源：[JKP · ni_ar1（Earnings persistence）](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ib→`n_income`（`financial_income`）；`close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：待核实（原JKP宏公式尚未完整展开；对应实现仅作参考）；条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；原非常项目前利润不等同中国净利润 |

| earnings_variability（Earnings variability）；PF01814 | 过去5年年度ROA=ni_x/at_x[-1年]，CROA=ocf_x/at_x[-1年]；std(ROA)/std(CROA)，两者均需5个有效年且CROA标准差&gt;0；[原算法](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors)；全部来源：[JKP · earnings_variability（Earnings variability）](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors) | ROA→`roa`（`financial_indicator`）；at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ib→`n_income`（`financial_income`）；oancf→`n_cashflow_act`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_indicator`）；`end_date`（`financial_indicator`） | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；原非常项目前利润不等同中国净利润 |
| aliq_at（Liquidity of book assets）；PF01815 | aliq_at=aliq_x / lag12(at_x)；aliq_x=che + 0.75 * coa_x + 0.5 * (at_x - ca_x - coalesce(intan, 0))；at_x=coalesce(at, seq_x + dltt + coalesce(lct, 0) + coalesce(lo, 0) + coalesce(txditc, 0))；coa_x=ca_x - che；ca_x=coalesce(act, rect+invt+che+aco)；seq_x=coalesce(seq, ceq+coalesce(pstk_x, 0), at-lt)；[原算法](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors)；全部来源：[JKP · aliq_at（Liquidity of book assets）](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；act→`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lt→`total_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lct→`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ceq→`total_hldr_eqy_exc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；che→`money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；invt→`inventories`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；rect→`accounts_receiv`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dltt→`lt_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；aco→`oth_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lo→`oth_ncl`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；intan→`intan_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；txditc→`defer_tax_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`） | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；需扣其他权益工具以确定普通股权益；原定义含短期投资，不只有money_cap；原长期债务范围比lt_borr宽，需加债券等；递延税与投资税抵免口径需核实 |
| aliq_mat（Liquidity of market assets）；PF01816 | aliq_mat=aliq_x * fx / lag12(mat)；aliq_x=che + 0.75 * coa_x + 0.5 * (at_x - ca_x - coalesce(intan, 0))；mat=at_x * fx - be_x * fx + me_company；coa_x=ca_x - che；at_x=coalesce(at, seq_x + dltt + coalesce(lct, 0) + coalesce(lo, 0) + coalesce(txditc, 0))；ca_x=coalesce(act, rect+invt+che+aco)；be_x=seq_x+coalesce(txditc_x, 0)-coalesce(pstk_x, 0)；[原算法](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors)；全部来源：[JKP · aliq_mat（Liquidity of market assets）](https://github.com/bkelly-lab/ReplicationCrisis/tree/67174c7f170bf8b952b3c86876f187fd9970b5ba/GlobalFactors) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；act→`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lct→`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ceq→`total_hldr_eqy_exc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；che→`money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；invt→`inventories`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；rect→`accounts_receiv`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dltt→`lt_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；aco→`oth_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lo→`oth_ncl`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；intan→`intan_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；txditc→`defer_tax_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；缺失：优先股账面价值 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：不支持原定义（缺上述输入）；已有字段只能形成另行命名的简化/适配版本 |

| betasq；PF01876 | 公开PyAnomaly对应定义（与原GKX窗口/口径尚未证明等价）：if created('pricedelay'):<br>    return；vars = cd[['ret', 'ym']].reset_index()；vars['yw'] = vars.date.dt.isocalendar().year * 100 + vars.date.dt.isocalendar().week；gb = vars.groupby(['permno', 'yw'])；reg = DataFrame(exp(gb.wkret.sum()) - 1)；reg['ym'] = gb.ym.nth(-1).to_numpy()；reg['ewret'] = reg.groupby(['yw']).wkret.transform('mean')；reg['ewret_l1'] = reg['ewret'].shift(1)；reg['ewret_l2'] = reg['ewret'].shift(2)；reg['ewret_l3'] = reg['ewret'].shift(3)；reg['ewret_l4'] = reg['ewret'].shift(4)；gb = reg.groupby('permno')；reg.loc[gb.cumcount() &lt; 4, ['ewret_l1', 'ewret_l2', 'ewret_l3', 'ewret_l4']] = nan；gsize = gb.size().to_numpy()；data = reg[['wkret', 'ewret', 'ewret_l1', 'ewret_l2', 'ewret_l3', 'ewret_l4']].to_numpy()；res = apply_to_groups_jit(data[:, :2], gsize, rolling_regression, 4, 156, 52, True)；reg['beta'] = res[:, 1]；reg['betasq'] = reg['beta'] ^ 2；reg['idiovol'] = res[:, -1]；r2 = res[:, -2]；res = apply_to_groups_jit(data, gsize, rolling_regression, 8, 156, 52, True)；beta2 = res[:, :-2]；r2_multi = res[:, -2]；reg['pricedelay'] = 1 - r2 / r2_multi；reg['pricedelay_slope'] = (beta2[:, 2:] * array([1, 2, 3, 4])).sum(1) / beta2[:, 1:].sum(1)；reg_ym = reg.reset_index().drop_duplicates(subset=['ym', 'permno'], keep='last')；reg_ym.index.names = ('date', 'permno')；ocolumns = ['beta', 'betasq', 'idiovol', 'pricedelay', 'pricedelay_slope']；data[ocolumns] = reg_ym.loc[:, ocolumns]；输出=data['betasq']；[参考算法](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3523)；全部来源：[GKX94 · betasq](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3523) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：待核定原GKX公式/窗口；支持（字段设计；历史完整性未核验）；原美股交易所/样本筛选不自动复制到中证500 |

| chatoia；PF01884 | 公开PyAnomaly对应定义（与原GKX窗口/口径尚未证明等价）：opat = rect + invt + aco + ppent + intan - ap - lco - lo；opat_avg = rolling(opat, 2, 'mean')；opat_avg[is_zero(opat_avg)] = nan；char = diff(sale / opat_avg)；输出=char；[参考算法](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1364)；全部来源：[GKX94 · chatoia](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1364) | invt→`inventories`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；rect→`accounts_receiv`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ap→`acct_payable`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ppent→`fix_assets_total`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；aco→`oth_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lco→`oth_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lo→`oth_ncl`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；intan→`intan_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；sale→`revenue`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：待核定原GKX公式/窗口；条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；需核对PP&amp;E范围；fix_assets_total不自动包含全部PP&amp;E |

| chmom；PF01888 | chmom=( (1+lag(ret))*(1+lag2(ret))*(1+lag3(ret))*(1+lag4(ret))*(1+lag5(ret))*(1+lag6(ret)) ) - 1 - (( (1+lag7(ret))*(1+lag8(ret))*(1+lag9(ret))*(1+lag10(ret))*(1+lag11(ret))*(1+lag12(ret)) ) - 1)；[参考算法](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars)；全部来源：[GKX94 · chmom](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：支持（字段设计；历史完整性未核验）；原美股交易所/样本筛选不自动复制到中证500 |

| cinvest；PF01891 | cinvest=((ppentq-lag(ppentq))/saleq)-mean(((lag(ppentq)-lag2(ppentq))/lag(saleq)),((lag2(ppentq)-lag3(ppentq))/lag2(saleq)),((lag3(ppentq)-lag4(ppentq))/lag3(saleq)))；[参考算法](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars)；全部来源：[GKX94 · cinvest](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars) | ppent→`fix_assets_total`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；sale→`revenue`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；需核对PP&amp;E范围；fix_assets_total不自动包含全部PP&amp;E |
| convind；PF01892 | 公开PyAnomaly对应定义（与原GKX窗口/口径尚未证明等价）：dc = dcvt.fillna(dcpstk - pstk.fillna(0))；dc[dc &lt; ZERO] = nan；char = (dc &gt; 0) &#124; (cshrc &gt; 0)；输出=char.astype(config.float_type)；[参考算法](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1434)；全部来源：[GKX94 · convind](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1434) | dc→`lt_amor_exp`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；缺失：优先股账面价值、可转债预留股数 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：待核定原GKX公式/窗口；不支持原定义（缺上述输入）；已有字段只能形成另行命名的简化/适配版本 |

| divi；PF01895 | 公开PyAnomaly对应定义（与原GKX窗口/口径尚未证明等价）：div = cash_div.fillna(0)；divinit = (div &gt; 0) &amp; (rolling(div, 24, 'sum', lag=1) == 0)；char = rolling(divinit, 6, 'sum') &gt; 0；输出=char.astype(config.float_type)；[参考算法](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L2429)；全部来源：[GKX94 · divi](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L2429) | `ann_date`（`financial_dividend`）；`imp_ann_date`（`financial_dividend`）；`ex_date`（`financial_dividend`）；`cash_div`（`financial_dividend`）；`div_proc`（`financial_dividend`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：待核定原GKX公式/窗口；条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；派息事件须按实施状态去重并保持公告时点；美股季度频率规则用于A股需适配 |
| divo；PF01896 | 公开PyAnomaly对应定义（与原GKX窗口/口径尚未证明等价）：div = cash_div.fillna(0)；div3 = lag(div, 3)；div6 = lag(div, 6)；div9 = lag(div, 9)；div12 = lag(div, 12)；div15 = lag(div, 15)；div18 = lag(div, 18)；div24 = lag(div, 24)；char1 = (div == 0) &amp; (div3 &gt; 0) &amp; (div6 &gt; 0) &amp; (div9 &gt; 0) &amp; (div12 &gt; 0) &amp; (div15 &gt; 0) &amp; (div18 &gt; 0)；char2 = (div == 0) &amp; (div3 == 0) &amp; (div6 &gt; 0) &amp; (div9 == 0) &amp; (div12 &gt; 0) &amp; (div15 == 0) &amp; (div18 &gt; 0)；char3 = (div == 0) &amp; (div3 == 0) &amp; (div6 == 0) &amp; (div9 == 0) &amp; (div12 &gt; 0) &amp; (div15 == 0) &amp; (div18 == 0) &amp; (div24 &gt; 0)；divomit = char1 &#124; char2 &#124; char3；char = rolling(divomit, 2, 'sum') &gt; 0；输出=char.astype(config.float_type)；[参考算法](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L2445)；全部来源：[GKX94 · divo](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L2445) | `ann_date`（`financial_dividend`）；`imp_ann_date`（`financial_dividend`）；`ex_date`（`financial_dividend`）；`cash_div`（`financial_dividend`）；`div_proc`（`financial_dividend`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：待核定原GKX公式/窗口；条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；派息事件须按实施状态去重并保持公告时点；美股季度频率规则用于A股需适配 |

| grltnoa；PF01904 | grltnoa=f_grltnoa；f_grltnoa=z_grltnoa；z_grltnoa=((rectq+invtq+ppentq+acoq+intanq+aoq-apq-lcoq-loq)-(lag4(rectq)+lag4(invtq)+lag4(ppentq)+lag4(acoq)+lag4(intanq)+lag4(aoq)-lag4(apq)-lag4(lcoq)-lag4(loq)) -( rectq-lag4(rectq)+invtq-lag4(invtq)+acoq-lag4(acoq)-(apq-lag4(apq)+lcoq-lag4(lcoq)) - %ttm4(dpq) ))/((atq+lag4(atq))/2)；[参考算法](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars)；全部来源：[GKX94 · grltnoa](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；invt→`inventories`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；rect→`accounts_receiv`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ap→`acct_payable`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ppent→`fix_assets_total`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；aco→`oth_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lco→`oth_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ao→`oth_nca`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lo→`oth_ncl`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；intan→`intan_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dp→`depr_fa_coga_dpba`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；需核对PP&amp;E范围；fix_assets_total不自动包含全部PP&amp;E |

| idiovol；PF01907 | 公开PyAnomaly对应定义（与原GKX窗口/口径尚未证明等价）：if created('pricedelay'):<br>    return；vars = cd[['ret', 'ym']].reset_index()；vars['yw'] = vars.date.dt.isocalendar().year * 100 + vars.date.dt.isocalendar().week；gb = vars.groupby(['permno', 'yw'])；reg = DataFrame(exp(gb.wkret.sum()) - 1)；reg['ym'] = gb.ym.nth(-1).to_numpy()；reg['ewret'] = reg.groupby(['yw']).wkret.transform('mean')；reg['ewret_l1'] = reg['ewret'].shift(1)；reg['ewret_l2'] = reg['ewret'].shift(2)；reg['ewret_l3'] = reg['ewret'].shift(3)；reg['ewret_l4'] = reg['ewret'].shift(4)；gb = reg.groupby('permno')；reg.loc[gb.cumcount() &lt; 4, ['ewret_l1', 'ewret_l2', 'ewret_l3', 'ewret_l4']] = nan；gsize = gb.size().to_numpy()；data = reg[['wkret', 'ewret', 'ewret_l1', 'ewret_l2', 'ewret_l3', 'ewret_l4']].to_numpy()；res = apply_to_groups_jit(data[:, :2], gsize, rolling_regression, 4, 156, 52, True)；reg['beta'] = res[:, 1]；reg['betasq'] = reg['beta'] ^ 2；reg['idiovol'] = res[:, -1]；r2 = res[:, -2]；res = apply_to_groups_jit(data, gsize, rolling_regression, 8, 156, 52, True)；beta2 = res[:, :-2]；r2_multi = res[:, -2]；reg['pricedelay'] = 1 - r2 / r2_multi；reg['pricedelay_slope'] = (beta2[:, 2:] * array([1, 2, 3, 4])).sum(1) / beta2[:, 1:].sum(1)；reg_ym = reg.reset_index().drop_duplicates(subset=['ym', 'permno'], keep='last')；reg_ym.index.names = ('date', 'permno')；ocolumns = ['beta', 'betasq', 'idiovol', 'pricedelay', 'pricedelay_slope']；data[ocolumns] = reg_ym.loc[:, ocolumns]；输出=data['idiovol']；[参考算法](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3529)；全部来源：[GKX94 · idiovol](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3529) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：待核定原GKX公式/窗口；支持（字段设计；历史完整性未核验）；原美股交易所/样本筛选不自动复制到中证500 |

| nincr；PF01921 | nincr=( (ibq&gt;lag(ibq)) + (ibq&gt;lag(ibq))*(lag(ibq)&gt;lag2(ibq)) + (ibq&gt;lag(ibq))*(lag(ibq)&gt;lag2(ibq))*(lag2(ibq)&gt;lag3(ibq)) + (ibq&gt;lag(ibq))*(lag(ibq)&gt;lag2(ibq))*(lag2(ibq)&gt;lag3(ibq))*(lag3(ibq)&gt;lag4(ibq)) + (ibq&gt;lag(ibq))*(lag(ibq)&gt;lag2(ibq))*(lag2(ibq)&gt;lag3(ibq))*(lag3(ibq)&gt;lag4(ibq))*(lag4(ibq)&gt;lag5(ibq)) + (ibq&gt;lag(ibq))*(lag(ibq)&gt;lag2(ibq))*(lag2(ibq)&gt;lag3(ibq))*(lag3(ibq)&gt;lag4(ibq))*(lag4(ibq)&gt;lag5(ibq))*(lag5(ibq)&gt;lag6(ibq)) + (ibq&gt;lag(ibq))*(lag(ibq)&gt;lag2(ibq))*(lag2(ibq)&gt;lag3(ibq))*(lag3(ibq)&gt;lag4(ibq))*(lag4(ibq)&gt;lag5(ibq))*(lag5(ibq)&gt;lag6(ibq))*(lag6(ibq)&gt;lag7(ibq)) + (ibq&gt;lag(ibq))*(lag(ibq)&gt;lag2(ibq))*(lag2(ibq)&gt;lag3(ibq))*(lag3(ibq)&gt;lag4(ibq))*(lag4(ibq)&gt;lag5(ibq))*(lag5(ibq)&gt;lag6(ibq))*(lag6(ibq)&gt;lag7(ibq))*(lag7(ibq)&gt;lag8(ibq)) )；[参考算法](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars)；全部来源：[GKX94 · nincr](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars) | ib→`n_income`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`） | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；原非常项目前利润不等同中国净利润 |
| operprof；PF01922 | operprof=(revt-cogs-xsga0-xint0)/lag(ceq)；xsga0=coalesce(xsga,0)；xint0=coalesce(xint,0)；[参考算法](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars)；全部来源：[GKX94 · operprof](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars) | ceq→`total_hldr_eqy_exc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；revt→`total_revenue`（`financial_income`）；cogs→`oper_cost`（`financial_income`）；xsga→`admin_exp`（`financial_income`）；`sell_exp`（`financial_income`）；xint→`fin_exp_int_exp`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`） | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；需扣其他权益工具以确定普通股权益；原SG&amp;A需与sell_exp+admin_exp及研发归属核对 |
| orgcap；PF01923 | OC_0=(xsga/CPI)/(0.10+0.15)，OC_t=0.85OC[-1年]+xsga/CPI；orgcap=OC/avg(at)；[参考算法](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars)；全部来源：[GKX94 · orgcap](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；xsga→`admin_exp`（`financial_income`）；`sell_exp`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；缺CPI | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：不支持原定义（缺CPI；本版使用CPI，不与OSAP的GNP平减指数混用） |
| pchcapx_ia；PF01924 | 公开PyAnomaly对应定义（与原GKX窗口/口径尚未证明等价）：capx = capx.fillna(diff(ppent))；fa['_pchcapx'] = _chg_to_exp(capx)；char = fa['_pchcapx'] - groupby(['sic2', 'fyear'])['_pchcapx'].transform('mean')；输出=char；[参考算法](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1443)；全部来源：[GKX94 · pchcapx_ia](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1443) | ppent→`fix_assets_total`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；capx→`c_pay_acq_const_fiolta`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`industry_sw_1`（`stock_day`）；`industry_sw_2`（`stock_day`）；`industry_sw_3`（`stock_day`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：待核定原GKX公式/窗口；条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；需核对PP&amp;E范围；fix_assets_total不自动包含全部PP&amp;E；购建固定/无形/长期资产现金比纯资本开支范围宽；申万分类不是SIC/NAICS/FF行业，需明确A股适配 |
| pchdepr；PF01926 | pchdepr=((dp/ppent)-(lag(dp)/lag(ppent)))/(lag(dp)/lag(ppent))；[参考算法](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars)；全部来源：[GKX94 · pchdepr](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars) | ppent→`fix_assets_total`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dp→`depr_fa_coga_dpba`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；需核对PP&amp;E范围；fix_assets_total不自动包含全部PP&amp;E |
| pchsale_pchxsga；PF01931 | pchsale_pchxsga=( (sale-lag(sale))/lag(sale) )-( (xsga-lag(xsga)) /lag(xsga) )；[参考算法](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars)；全部来源：[GKX94 · pchsale_pchxsga](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars) | sale→`revenue`（`financial_income`）；xsga→`admin_exp`（`financial_income`）；`sell_exp`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原数学定义、原始科目映射、原样本过滤或完整计算/缺失回退规则尚未逐项核定；暂不列为可直接实现因子，不以简化近似冒充原项 |
| pctacc；PF01933 | pctacc=f_pctacc；f_pctacc=z_pctacc；z_pctacc=((ACTq-LCTq+NPq)-(lag4(ACTq)-lag4(LCTq)+lag4(NPq)))/abs(%ttm4(ibq))；[参考算法](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars)；全部来源：[GKX94 · pctacc](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars) | act→`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lct→`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ib→`n_income`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`） | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；原非常项目前利润不等同中国净利润 |
| pricedelay；PF01934 | 公开PyAnomaly对应定义（与原GKX窗口/口径尚未证明等价）：if created('pricedelay'):<br>    return；vars = cd[['ret', 'ym']].reset_index()；vars['yw'] = vars.date.dt.isocalendar().year * 100 + vars.date.dt.isocalendar().week；gb = vars.groupby(['permno', 'yw'])；reg = DataFrame(exp(gb.wkret.sum()) - 1)；reg['ym'] = gb.ym.nth(-1).to_numpy()；reg['ewret'] = reg.groupby(['yw']).wkret.transform('mean')；reg['ewret_l1'] = reg['ewret'].shift(1)；reg['ewret_l2'] = reg['ewret'].shift(2)；reg['ewret_l3'] = reg['ewret'].shift(3)；reg['ewret_l4'] = reg['ewret'].shift(4)；gb = reg.groupby('permno')；reg.loc[gb.cumcount() &lt; 4, ['ewret_l1', 'ewret_l2', 'ewret_l3', 'ewret_l4']] = nan；gsize = gb.size().to_numpy()；data = reg[['wkret', 'ewret', 'ewret_l1', 'ewret_l2', 'ewret_l3', 'ewret_l4']].to_numpy()；res = apply_to_groups_jit(data[:, :2], gsize, rolling_regression, 4, 156, 52, True)；reg['beta'] = res[:, 1]；reg['betasq'] = reg['beta'] ^ 2；reg['idiovol'] = res[:, -1]；r2 = res[:, -2]；res = apply_to_groups_jit(data, gsize, rolling_regression, 8, 156, 52, True)；beta2 = res[:, :-2]；r2_multi = res[:, -2]；reg['pricedelay'] = 1 - r2 / r2_multi；reg['pricedelay_slope'] = (beta2[:, 2:] * array([1, 2, 3, 4])).sum(1) / beta2[:, 1:].sum(1)；reg_ym = reg.reset_index().drop_duplicates(subset=['ym', 'permno'], keep='last')；reg_ym.index.names = ('date', 'permno')；ocolumns = ['beta', 'betasq', 'idiovol', 'pricedelay', 'pricedelay_slope']；data[ocolumns] = reg_ym.loc[:, ocolumns]；输出=data['pricedelay']；[参考算法](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3536)；全部来源：[GKX94 · pricedelay](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3536) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：待核定原GKX公式/窗口；支持（字段设计；历史完整性未核验）；原美股交易所/样本筛选不自动复制到中证500 |

| stdcf；PF01958 | stdcf=std(scf,lag(scf),lag2(scf),lag3(scf),lag4(scf),lag5(scf),lag6(scf),lag7(scf), lag8(scf),lag9(scf),lag10(scf),lag11(scf),lag12(scf),lag13(scf),lag14(scf),lag15(scf))；scf=(ibq/saleq)-sacc；sacc=( (actq-lag(actq) - (cheq-lag(cheq))) - ( (lctq-lag(lctq))-(dlcq-lag(dlcq)) ) ) /saleq；[参考算法](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars)；全部来源：[GKX94 · stdcf](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars) | act→`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lct→`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；che→`money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dlc→`st_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；sale→`revenue`（`financial_income`）；ib→`n_income`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`） | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；原定义含短期投资，不只有money_cap；原一年内到期债务需加non_cur_liab_due_1y等；原非常项目前利润不等同中国净利润 |
| tang；PF01959 | tang=(che+rect*0.715+invt*0.547+ppent*0.535)/at；[参考算法](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars)；全部来源：[GKX94 · tang](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；che→`money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；invt→`inventories`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；rect→`accounts_receiv`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ppent→`fix_assets_total`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；原定义含短期投资，不只有money_cap；需核对PP&amp;E范围；fix_assets_total不自动包含全部PP&amp;E |
| tb；PF01960 | 公开PyAnomaly对应定义（与原GKX窗口/口径尚未证明等价）：tax_rate = Series(0.35, index=index)；tax_rate[fyear &lt; 1993] = 0.34；tax_rate[fyear == 1987] = 0.4；tax_rate[fyear &lt; 1987] = 0.46；tax_rate[fyear &lt; 1979] = 0.48；numer = (txfo + txfed).fillna(txt - txdi)；char = numer / (tax_rate * ib)；char[(numer &gt; 0) &amp; (ib &lt;= ZERO)] = 1；char[(numer &lt;= 0) &amp; (ib &lt;= ZERO)] = nan；输出=char；[参考算法](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1452)；全部来源：[GKX94 · tb](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1452) | ib→`n_income`（`financial_income`）；txt→`income_tax`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；缺失：外国所得税、联邦所得税、递延所得税费用 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：待核定原GKX公式/窗口；不支持原定义（缺上述输入）；已有字段只能形成另行命名的简化/适配版本 |
| turn；PF01961 | turn=mean(lag(vol),lag2(vol),lag3(vol))/shrout；[参考算法](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars)；全部来源：[GKX94 · turn](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；`total_mv`（`stock_day`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：支持（字段设计；历史完整性未核验）；原美股交易所/样本筛选不自动复制到中证500 |
| zerotrade；PF01962 | 零成交天数与换手率平局修正项组成的Liu流动性指标；精确月窗口按原GHZ版本核定；[参考算法](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars)；全部来源：[GKX94 · zerotrade](https://github.com/Feng-CityUHK/EquityCharacteristicsSAS/tree/7a3c428417664720ea4a59653947504834018089/chars) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；`vol`（`stock_day`）；`amount`（`stock_day`）；`total_share`（`stock_day`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：待核定原GKX公式/窗口；支持（字段设计；历史完整性未核验）；原美股交易所/样本筛选不自动复制到中证500 |

| cvturn[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（Coefficient of Variation of Share Turnover, 6month）；PF01969 | volume = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dnshrtrd')；mv = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dsmvosd')；price = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Clsprc')；number = mv / price；turnover = volume / number；sd = turnover.rolling(trading_day_num, min_periods=min_day_num).std()；mean = turnover.rolling(trading_day_num, min_periods=min_day_num).mean()；result = sd / mean；result['Trdmnt'] = list(result.index)；result.Trdmnt = result.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L255)；全部来源：[A股特征 · cvturn[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（Coefficient of Variation of Share Turnover, 6month）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L255) | `vol`（`stock_day`）；`circ_mv`（`stock_day`）；`close`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Dnshrtrd;TRD_Dalyr-Dsmvosd;TRD_Dalyr-Clsprc | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原数学定义、原始科目映射、原样本过滤或完整计算/缺失回退规则尚未逐项核定；暂不列为可直接实现因子，不以简化近似冒充原项 |

| cvd[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（Coefficient of Variation of Dollar Trading Volume）；PF01975 | volume = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dnvaltrd')；sd = volume.rolling(trading_day_num, min_periods=min_day_num).std()；mean = volume.rolling(trading_day_num, min_periods=min_day_num).mean()；result = sd / mean；result['Trdmnt'] = list(result.index)；result.Trdmnt = result.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L325)；全部来源：[A股特征 · cvd[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（Coefficient of Variation of Dollar Trading Volume）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L325) | `amount`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Dnvaltrd | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原数学定义、原始科目映射、原样本过滤或完整计算/缺失回退规则尚未逐项核定；暂不列为可直接实现因子，不以简化近似冒充原项 |
| ami（Amihud Illiquidity）；PF01976 | volume = get_data('TRD_Mnth', 'Mnvaltrd')；ret = abs(monthly_ret.copy())；Ami = ret / volume；输出=Ami；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L339)；全部来源：[A股特征 · ami（Amihud Illiquidity）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L339) | `amount`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Mnth-Mnvaltrd | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原数学定义、原始科目映射、原样本过滤或完整计算/缺失回退规则尚未逐项核定；暂不列为可直接实现因子，不以简化近似冒充原项 |
| idvc[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（idiosyncratic volatility(CAPM)）；PF01977 | daily_ret = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dretwd')；market_ret = get_data('TRD_Cndalym', 'Cdretwdos')；result = daily_ret.copy()；result.iloc[:, :] = nan；rf = get_data('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')；market_and_rf = market_ret.merge(rf, on=['Trddt'])；rf = array(market_and_rf.iloc[:, -1])；x = array(market_and_rf.iloc[:, -2]) - rf；x = sm.add_constant(x)；for j in range(result.shape[1]):<br>    y = array(daily_ret.iloc[:, j]) - rf<br>    model = RollingOLS(y, x, window=trading_day_num, min_nobs=min_day_num).fit()<br>    result.iloc[:, j] = sqrt(model.mse_resid * model.df_resid / (model.df_model + 1 + model.df_resid))；result['Trdmnt'] = list(result.index)；result.Trdmnt = result.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L350)；全部来源：[A股特征 · idvc[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（idiosyncratic volatility(CAPM)）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L350) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：股票日度收益率：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd；市场日收益率：TRD_Cndalym-Cdretwdos；未对应原输入：[('TRD_Cndalym', 'Cdretwdos'), ('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')]；可选中证500替代基准：`close`（`zz500_daily_index`），不是原全市场收益 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| idvff[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（idiosyncratic volatility(FF3)）；PF01978 | daily_ret = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dretwd')；ff3 = get_data('STK_MKT_THRFACDAY')；result = daily_ret.copy()；result.iloc[:, :] = nan；rf = get_data('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')；ff3_and_rf = ff3.merge(rf, on=['Trddt'])；rf = array(ff3_and_rf.iloc[:, -1])；x = array(ff3_and_rf.iloc[:, -4:-1])；x = sm.add_constant(x)；for j in range(result.shape[1]):<br>    y = array(daily_ret.iloc[:, j]) - rf<br>    model = RollingOLS(y, x, window=trading_day_num, min_nobs=min_day_num).fit()<br>    result.iloc[:, j] = sqrt(model.mse_resid * model.df_resid / (model.df_model + 1 + model.df_resid))；result['Trdmnt'] = list(result.index)；result.Trdmnt = result.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L377)；全部来源：[A股特征 · idvff[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（idiosyncratic volatility(FF3)）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L377) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd;STK_MKT_THRFACDAY;TRD_Nrrate-Nrrdaydt；未对应原输入：[('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| idvq[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（Idiosyncratic Volatility per the q-factor Model）；PF01979 | daily_ret = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dretwd')；q_factor = get_data('STK_MKT_FIVEFACDAY')；result = daily_ret.copy()；result.iloc[:, :] = nan；q_factor = daily_ret.merge(q_factor, how='outer', on=['Trddt'])；q_factor = q_factor[['Trddt', 'RiskPremium1', 'SMB1', 'RMW1', 'CMA1']]；rf = get_data('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')；q_factor_and_rf = q_factor.merge(rf, on=['Trddt'])；rf = array(q_factor_and_rf.iloc[:, -1])；x = array(q_factor_and_rf.iloc[:, -5:-1])；x = sm.add_constant(x)；for j in range(result.shape[1]):<br>    y = array(daily_ret.iloc[:, j]) - rf<br>    model = RollingOLS(y, x, window=trading_day_num, min_nobs=min_day_num).fit()<br>    result.iloc[:, j] = sqrt(model.mse_resid * model.df_resid / (model.df_model + 1 + model.df_resid))；result['Trdmnt'] = list(result.index)；result.Trdmnt = result.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L408)；全部来源：[A股特征 · idvq[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（Idiosyncratic Volatility per the q-factor Model）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L408) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd;STK_MKT_FIVEFACDAY;TRD_Nrrate-Nrrdaydt；未对应原输入：[('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| tv[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（total volatility）；PF01980 | daily_ret = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dretwd')；result = daily_ret.rolling(trading_day_num, min_periods=min_day_num).std()；result['Trdmnt'] = list(result.index)；result.Trdmnt = result.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L442)；全部来源：[A股特征 · tv[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（total volatility）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L442) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：股票日度收益率：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原数学定义、原始科目映射、原样本过滤或完整计算/缺失回退规则尚未逐项核定；暂不列为可直接实现因子，不以简化近似冒充原项 |
| idsff[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（idiosyncratic skewness(FF3)）；PF01981 | daily_ret = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dretwd')；ff3 = get_data('STK_MKT_THRFACDAY')；rf = get_data('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')；ff3_and_rf = ff3.merge(rf, on=['Trddt'])；rf = array(ff3_and_rf.iloc[:, -1])；x = array(ff3_and_rf.iloc[:, -4:-1])；x = sm.add_constant(x)；sk = daily_ret.copy()；sk.iloc[:, :] = nan；sk['Trdmnt'] = list(sk.index)；sk.Trdmnt = sk.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；for j in range(sk.shape[1] - 1):<br>    y = array(daily_ret.iloc[:, j]) - rf<br>    model = RollingOLS(y, x, window=trading_day_num, min_nobs=min_day_num).fit()<br>    iv = sqrt(model.mse_resid * model.df_resid / (model.df_model + 1 + model.df_resid))<br>    num = model.df_model + 1 + model.df_resid<br>    for m in range(sk.shape[0]):<br>        pos = list(daily_ret.index).index(sk.index[m])<br>        temp_x = x[pos - trading_day_num:pos, :]<br>        temp_y = y[pos - trading_day_num:pos]<br>        temp_param = model.params[pos]<br>        valid_list = ~isnan(temp_y)<br>        temp_y = temp_y[valid_list]<br>        temp_x = temp_x[valid_list]<br>        temp_pre = dot(temp_x, temp_param)<br>        temp_res = temp_y - temp_pre<br>        temp_iv = iv[pos]<br>        temp_num = num[pos]<br>        temp_is = sum(power(temp_res, 3)) / (temp_num * power(temp_iv, 3))<br>        sk.iloc[m, j] = temp_is；输出=sk；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L453)；全部来源：[A股特征 · idsff[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（idiosyncratic skewness(FF3)）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L453) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd;STK_MKT_THRFACDAY;TRD_Nrrate-Nrrdaydt；未对应原输入：[('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |

rf；x = sm.add_constant(x)；sk = daily_ret.copy()；sk.iloc[:, :] = nan；sk['Trdmnt'] = list(sk.index)；sk.Trdmnt = sk.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；for j in range(sk.shape[1] - 1):<br>    y = array(daily_ret.iloc[:, j]) - rf<br>    model = RollingOLS(y, x, window=trading_day_num, min_nobs=min_day_num).fit()<br>    iv = sqrt(model.mse_resid * model.df_resid / (model.df_model + 1 + model.df_resid))<br>    num = model.df_model + 1 + model.df_resid<br>    for m in range(sk.shape[0]):<br>        pos = list(daily_ret.index).index(sk.index[m])<br>        temp_x = x[pos - trading_day_num:pos, :]<br>        temp_y = y[pos - trading_day_num:pos]<br>        temp_param = model.params[pos]<br>        valid_list = ~isnan(temp_y)<br>        temp_y = temp_y[valid_list]<br>        temp_x = temp_x[valid_list]<br>        temp_pre = dot(temp_x, temp_param)<br>        temp_res = temp_y - temp_pre<br>        temp_iv = iv[pos]<br>        temp_num = num[pos]<br>        temp_is = sum(power(temp_res, 3)) / (temp_num * power(temp_iv, 3))<br>        sk.iloc[m, j] = temp_is；输出=sk；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L550)；全部来源：[A股特征 · idsc[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（idiosyncratic skewness(CAPM)）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L550) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd;TRD_Cndalym-Cdretwdos;TRD_Nrrate-Nrrdaydt；未对应原输入：[('TRD_Cndalym', 'Cdretwdos'), ('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')]；可选中证500替代基准：`close`（`zz500_daily_index`），不是原全市场收益 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| ts[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（total skewness）；PF01983 | daily_ret = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dretwd')；result = daily_ret.rolling(trading_day_num, min_periods=min_day_num).skew()；result['Trdmnt'] = list(result.index)；result.Trdmnt = result.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L595)；全部来源：[A股特征 · ts[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（total skewness）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L595) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原数学定义、原始科目映射、原样本过滤或完整计算/缺失回退规则尚未逐项核定；暂不列为可直接实现因子，不以简化近似冒充原项 |
| cs[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（co-skewness）；PF01984 | daily_ret = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dretwd')；market_ret = get_data('TRD_Cndalym', 'Cdretwdos')；rf = get_data('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')；market_and_rf = market_ret.merge(rf, on=['Trddt'])；rf = array(market_and_rf.iloc[:, -1])；x = array(market_and_rf.iloc[:, -2]) - rf；x = sm.add_constant(x)；cs = daily_ret.copy()；cs.iloc[:, :] = nan；cs['Trdmnt'] = list(cs.index)；cs.Trdmnt = cs.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；for j in range(cs.shape[1] - 1):<br>    y = array(daily_ret.iloc[:, j]) - rf<br>    model = RollingOLS(y, x, window=trading_day_num, min_nobs=min_day_num).fit()<br>    for m in range(cs.shape[0]):<br>        pos = list(daily_ret.index).index(cs.index[m])<br>        temp_x = x[pos - trading_day_num:pos, :]<br>        temp_y = y[pos - trading_day_num:pos]<br>        temp_param = model.params[pos]<br>        valid_list = ~isnan(temp_y)<br>        temp_y = temp_y[valid_list]<br>        temp_x = temp_x[valid_list]<br>        temp_pre = dot(temp_x, temp_param)<br>        temp_res = temp_y - temp_pre<br>        temp_mkt = temp_x[:, -1] - nanmean(temp_x[:, -1])<br>        temp_cs = nanmean(temp_res * temp_mkt * temp_mkt) / (sqrt(nanmean(temp_res * temp_res)) * nanmean(temp_mkt * temp_mkt))<br>        cs.iloc[m, j] = temp_cs；输出=cs；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L605)；全部来源：[A股特征 · cs[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（co-skewness）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L605) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd;TRD_Cndalym-Cdretwdos;TRD_Nrrate-Nrrdaydt；未对应原输入：[('TRD_Cndalym', 'Cdretwdos'), ('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')]；可选中证500替代基准：`close`（`zz500_daily_index`），不是原全市场收益 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| beta[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（beta）；PF01985 | daily_ret = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dretwd')；market_ret = get_data('TRD_Cndalym', 'Cdretwdos')；result = daily_ret.copy()；result.iloc[:, :] = nan；rf = get_data('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')；market_and_rf = market_ret.merge(rf, on=['Trddt'])；rf = array(market_and_rf.iloc[:, -1])；x = array(market_and_rf.iloc[:, -2]) - rf；x = sm.add_constant(x)；for j in range(result.shape[1]):<br>    y = array(daily_ret.iloc[:, j]) - rf<br>    model = RollingOLS(y, x, window=trading_day_num, min_nobs=min_day_num).fit()<br>    result.iloc[:, j] = model.params[:, -1]；result['Trdmnt'] = list(result.index)；result.Trdmnt = result.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L646)；全部来源：[A股特征 · beta[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（beta）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L646) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：股票日度收益率：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd；市场日收益率：TRD_Cndalym-Cdretwdos；未对应原输入：[('TRD_Cndalym', 'Cdretwdos'), ('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')]；可选中证500替代基准：`close`（`zz500_daily_index`），不是原全市场收益 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| dbeta[trading_day_num=240, min_day_num=80]（downside beta）；PF01986 | daily_ret = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dretwd')；market_ret = get_data('TRD_Cndalym', 'Cdretwdos')；rf = get_data('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')；market_and_rf = market_ret.merge(rf, on=['Trddt'])；rf = array(market_and_rf.iloc[:, -1])；mkt = array(market_and_rf.iloc[:, -2]) - rf；dbeta = daily_ret.copy()；dbeta.iloc[:, :] = nan；dbeta['Trdmnt'] = list(dbeta.index)；dbeta.Trdmnt = dbeta.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；for j in range(dbeta.shape[1] - 1):<br>    y = array(daily_ret.iloc[:, j]) - rf<br>    for m in range(dbeta.shape[0]):<br>        pos = list(daily_ret.index).index(dbeta.index[m])<br>        temp_mkt = mkt[pos - trading_day_num:pos]<br>        temp_y = y[pos - trading_day_num:pos]<br>        valid_list = ~isnan(temp_y)<br>        temp_y = temp_y[valid_list]<br>        temp_mkt = temp_mkt[valid_list]<br>        down_list = temp_mkt &lt; nanmean(temp_mkt)<br>        temp_y = temp_y[down_list]<br>        temp_mkt = temp_mkt[down_list]<br>        if len(temp_y) &gt;= min_day_num:<br>            dbeta.iloc[m, j] = cov(temp_y, temp_mkt)[0][1] / var(temp_mkt)<br>        else:<br>            dbeta.iloc[m, j] = nan；输出=dbeta；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L753)；全部来源：[A股特征 · dbeta[trading_day_num=240, min_day_num=80]（downside beta）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L753) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd;TRD_Cndalym-Cdretwdos;TRD_Nrrate-Nrrdaydt；未对应原输入：[('TRD_Cndalym', 'Cdretwdos'), ('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')]；可选中证500替代基准：`close`（`zz500_daily_index`），不是原全市场收益 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| betafp[win=120, minn=40]（frazzni-pedersen beta）；PF01987 | daily_ret = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dretwd')；market_ret = get_data('TRD_Cndalym', 'Cdretwdos')；rf = get_data('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')；market_and_rf = market_ret.merge(rf, on=['Trddt'])；rf = array(market_and_rf.iloc[:, -1])；mkt = array(market_and_rf.iloc[:, -2]) - rf；daily_excess = daily_ret.apply(lambda x: x - rf)；daily_sigma = daily_logexcess.rolling(win, min_periods=minn).std()；mkt_sigma = array(DataFrame(mkt).rolling(win, min_periods=minn).std())；r3d = daily_logexcess.rolling(3).sum()；betafp = daily_ret.copy()；betafp.iloc[:, :] = nan；betafp['Trdmnt'] = list(betafp.index)；betafp.Trdmnt = betafp.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；for j in range(betafp.shape[1] - 1):<br>    for m in range(betafp.shape[0]):<br>        pos = list(daily_ret.index).index(betafp.index[m])<br>        temp_daily_sigma = daily_sigma.iloc[pos, j]<br>        temp_mkt_sigma = mkt_sigma[pos][0]<br>        temp_mkt = mkt[pos - win:pos]<br>        temp_r3d = array(r3d.iloc[pos - win:pos, j])<br>        valid_list = ~isnan(temp_r3d)<br>        temp_r3d = temp_r3d[valid_list]<br>        temp_mkt = temp_mkt[valid_list]<br>        if len(temp_r3d) &gt;= minn:<br>            betafp.iloc[m, j] = corrcoef(temp_r3d, temp_mkt)[0][1] * temp_daily_sigma / temp_mkt_sigma<br>        else:<br>            betafp.iloc[m, j] = nan；输出=betafp；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L794)；全部来源：[A股特征 · betafp[win=120, minn=40]（frazzni-pedersen beta）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L794) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd;TRD_Cndalym-Cdretwdos;TRD_Nrrate-Nrrdaydt；未对应原输入：[('TRD_Cndalym', 'Cdretwdos'), ('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')]；可选中证500替代基准：`close`（`zz500_daily_index`），不是原全市场收益 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| betadm[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（dimson beta）；PF01988 | daily_ret = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dretwd')；market_ret = get_data('TRD_Cndalym', 'Cdretwdos')；result = daily_ret.copy()；result.iloc[:, :] = nan；rf = get_data('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')；market_and_rf = market_ret.merge(rf, on=['Trddt'])；rf = array(market_and_rf.iloc[:, -1])；market_and_rf.iloc[:, -2] = market_and_rf.iloc[:, -2] - rf；x1 = array(market_and_rf.iloc[:, -2])；x2 = array(market_and_rf.shift(-1).iloc[:, -2])；x3 = array(market_and_rf.shift(1).iloc[:, -2])；x = array([x1, x2, x3]).T；x = sm.add_constant(x)；for j in range(result.shape[1]):<br>    y = array(daily_ret.iloc[:, j]) - rf<br>    if sum(~isnan(y)) &gt;= 10:<br>        model = RollingOLS(y, x, window=trading_day_num, min_nobs=min_day_num).fit()<br>        result.iloc[:, j] = model.params[:, -1] + model.params[:, -2] + model.params[:, -3]；result['Trdmnt'] = list(result.index)；result.Trdmnt = result.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L838)；全部来源：[A股特征 · betadm[trading_day_num=120, min_day_num=40]（dimson beta）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L838) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd;TRD_Cndalym-Cdretwdos;TRD_Nrrate-Nrrdaydt；未对应原输入：[('TRD_Cndalym', 'Cdretwdos'), ('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')]；可选中证500替代基准：`close`（`zz500_daily_index`），不是原全市场收益 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| tail[trading_month_num=12, min_month_num=6]（Tail Risk）；PF01989 | daily_ret = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dretwd')；market_ret = get_data('TRD_Cndalym', 'Cdretwdos')；x = daily_ret.copy()；x.iloc[:, :] = nan；x['Trdmnt'] = list(x.index)；x.Trdmnt = x.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；rf = get_data('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')；market_and_rf = market_ret.merge(rf, on=['Trddt'])；market_and_rf['Trdmnt'] = list(market_and_rf.index)；market_and_rf.Trdmnt = market_and_rf.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；rf = array(market_and_rf.iloc[:, -1])；ret = monthly_ret.copy()；y = y.shift(1)；y = sm.add_constant(y)；result = monthly_ret.copy()；result.iloc[:, :] = nan；for j in range(result.shape[1]):<br>    y = array(ret.iloc[:, j]) - rf<br>    model = RollingOLS(y, x, window=trading_month_num, min_nobs=min_month_num).fit()<br>    result[:, j] = model.params[:-1]；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L872)；全部来源：[A股特征 · tail[trading_month_num=12, min_month_num=6]（Tail Risk）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L872) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd;TRD_Cndalym-Cdretwdos;TRD_Nrrate-Nrrdaydt；未对应原输入：[('TRD_Cndalym', 'Cdretwdos'), ('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')]；可选中证500替代基准：`close`（`zz500_daily_index`），不是原全市场收益 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |

| m6[start=1, end=5]（6 month momentum）；PF01993 | ret = monthly_ret.copy()；result = 1；for i in range(start, end + 1):<br>    temp_lag = ret.shift(i)<br>    result = result * (1 + temp_lag)；result = result - 1；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L933)；全部来源：[A股特征 · m6[start=1, end=5]（6 month momentum）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L933) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；原CSMAR依赖：TRD_Mnth-Mretwd | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：支持（字段设计；历史完整性未核验）；原美股交易所/样本筛选不自动复制到中证500 |

| indmom[month_num=6, min_month_num=2]（industry momentum）；PF01995 | ind_table = get_data('TRD_Co', 'Nnindcd')；stock_ind = monthly_ret.copy()；for i in range(stock_ind.shape[1]):<br>    ind = list(ind_table.loc[stock_ind.columns[i]])[0]<br>    stock_ind.iloc[:, i] = [ind] * stock_ind.shape[0]；universe = monthly_ret.copy()；universe = isnan(universe)；stock_ind[universe] = nan；ret = monthly_ret.copy()；rs = ret.rolling(month_num, min_periods=min_month_num).mean()；mv = get_data('TRD_Mnth', 'Msmvosd')；sqmv = sqrt(mv)；ind_list = list(set(list(ind_table.iloc[:, 0])))；indmom = monthly_ret.copy()；indmom.iloc[:, :] = nan；rsi = indmom.copy()；for i in range(stock_ind.shape[0]):<br>    for j in ind_list:<br>        temp = stock_ind.iloc[i, :]<br>        pos = where(temp == j)[0]<br>        temp_sqmv = array(sqmv.iloc[i, pos])<br>        temp_rs = array(rs.iloc[i, pos])<br>        temp_rsi = nansum(temp_rs * temp_sqmv) / nansum(temp_sqmv)<br>        rsi.iloc[i, pos] = temp_rsi；indmom = -(sqmv * rs - rsi)；输出=indmom；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L962)；全部来源：[A股特征 · indmom[month_num=6, min_month_num=2]（industry momentum）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L962) | `industry_sw_1`（`stock_day`）；`circ_mv`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：股票月度收益率：TRD_Mnth-Mretwd；市值：TRD_Mnth-Msmvosd；行业：TRD_Co-Nnindcd | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原数学定义、原始科目映射、原样本过滤或完整计算/缺失回退规则尚未逐项核定；暂不列为可直接实现因子，不以简化近似冒充原项 |
| m24[start=12, end=35]（24 month momentum）；PF01996 | ret = monthly_ret.copy()；skip = isnan(ret)；ret[skip] = 0；result = 1；for i in range(start, end + 1):<br>    temp_lag = ret.shift(i)<br>    result = result * (1 + temp_lag)；result = result - 1；result[skip] = nan；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L999)；全部来源：[A股特征 · m24[start=12, end=35]（24 month momentum）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L999) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；原CSMAR依赖：TRD_Mnth-Mretwd | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：支持（字段设计；历史完整性未核验）；原美股交易所/样本筛选不自动复制到中证500 |
| mchg[s1=1, e1=6, s2=7, e2=12]（momentum change）；PF01997 | ret = monthly_ret.copy()；m1 = 1；for i in range(s1, e1 + 1):<br>    temp_lag = ret.shift(i)<br>    m1 = m1 * (1 + temp_lag)；m1 = m1 - 1；m2 = 1；for i in range(s2, e2 + 1):<br>    temp_lag = ret.shift(i)<br>    m2 = m2 * (1 + temp_lag)；m2 = m2 - 1；result = m1 - m2；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1014)；全部来源：[A股特征 · mchg[s1=1, e1=6, s2=7, e2=12]（momentum change）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1014) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；原CSMAR依赖：TRD_Mnth-Mretwd | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：支持（字段设计；历史完整性未核验）；原美股交易所/样本筛选不自动复制到中证500 |
| im12[im_day_num=240, trading_day_num=720, min_day_num=240]（12 month residual momentum）；PF01998 | daily_ret = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dretwd')；ff3 = get_data('STK_MKT_THRFACDAY')；rf = get_data('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')；ff3_and_rf = ff3.merge(rf, on=['Trddt'])；rf = array(ff3_and_rf.iloc[:, -1])；x = array(ff3_and_rf.iloc[:, -4:-1])；x = sm.add_constant(x)；im = daily_ret.copy()；im.iloc[:, :] = nan；im['Trdmnt'] = list(im.index)；im.Trdmnt = im.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；for j in range(im.shape[1] - 1):<br>    y = array(daily_ret.iloc[:, j]) - rf<br>    model = RollingOLS(y, x, window=trading_day_num, min_nobs=min_day_num).fit()<br>    for m in range(im.shape[0]):<br>        pos = list(daily_ret.index).index(im.index[m])<br>        temp_x = x[pos - im_day_num:pos, :]<br>        temp_y = y[pos - im_day_num:pos]<br>        temp_param = model.params[pos]<br>        valid_list = ~isnan(temp_y)<br>        temp_y = temp_y[valid_list]<br>        temp_x = temp_x[valid_list]<br>        temp_pre = dot(temp_x, temp_param)<br>        temp_res = temp_y - temp_pre<br>        if nanstd(temp_res) != 0:<br>            im.iloc[m, j] = nanmean(temp_res) / nanstd(temp_res)<br>        else:<br>            im.iloc[m, j] = nan；输出=im；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1033)；全部来源：[A股特征 · im12[im_day_num=240, trading_day_num=720, min_day_num=240]（12 month residual momentum）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1033) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd;STK_MKT_THRFACDAY;TRD_Nrrate-Nrrdaydt；未对应原输入：[('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| im6[im_day_num=120, trading_day_num=720, min_day_num=240]（6 month residual momentum）；PF01999 | daily_ret = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dretwd')；ff3 = get_data('STK_MKT_THRFACDAY')；rf = get_data('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')；ff3_and_rf = ff3.merge(rf, on=['Trddt'])；rf = array(ff3_and_rf.iloc[:, -1])；x = array(ff3_and_rf.iloc[:, -4:-1])；x = sm.add_constant(x)；im = daily_ret.copy()；im.iloc[:, :] = nan；im['Trdmnt'] = list(im.index)；im.Trdmnt = im.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；for j in range(im.shape[1] - 1):<br>    y = array(daily_ret.iloc[:, j]) - rf<br>    model = RollingOLS(y, x, window=trading_day_num, min_nobs=min_day_num).fit()<br>    for m in range(im.shape[0]):<br>        pos = list(daily_ret.index).index(im.index[m])<br>        temp_x = x[pos - im_day_num:pos, :]<br>        temp_y = y[pos - im_day_num:pos]<br>        temp_param = model.params[pos]<br>        valid_list = ~isnan(temp_y)<br>        temp_y = temp_y[valid_list]<br>        temp_x = temp_x[valid_list]<br>        temp_pre = dot(temp_x, temp_param)<br>        temp_res = temp_y - temp_pre<br>        if nanstd(temp_res) != 0:<br>            im.iloc[m, j] = nanmean(temp_res) / nanstd(temp_res)<br>        else:<br>            im.iloc[m, j] = nan；输出=im；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1074)；全部来源：[A股特征 · im6[im_day_num=120, trading_day_num=720, min_day_num=240]（6 month residual momentum）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1074) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd;STK_MKT_THRFACDAY;TRD_Nrrate-Nrrdaydt；未对应原输入：[('TRD_Nrrate', 'Nrrdaydt')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| 52w（52-week high）；PF02000 | daily = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Adjprcwd')；result = daily.copy()；result.iloc[:, :] = nan；result['Trdmnt'] = list(result.index)；result.Trdmnt = result.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；for j in range(result.shape[1] - 1):<br>    for m in range(result.shape[0]):<br>        pos = list(daily.index).index(result.index[m])<br>        temp = array(daily.iloc[pos - 240:pos, j])<br>        if len(temp[~isnan(temp)]) &gt; 0:<br>            result.iloc[m, j] = temp[-1] / max(temp[~isnan(temp)])；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1116)；全部来源：[A股特征 · 52w（52-week high）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1116) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Adjprcwd | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原数学定义、原始科目映射、原样本过滤或完整计算/缺失回退规则尚未逐项核定；暂不列为可直接实现因子，不以简化近似冒充原项 |
| mdr[mean_num=5, min_day_num=15, min_feb_num=5]（maximum daily return ）；PF02001 | daily_ret = get_data('TRD_Dalyr', 'Dretwd')；daily_ret['Trdmnt'] = list(daily_ret.index)；daily_ret.Trdmnt = daily_ret.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；result = monthly_ret.copy()；result.iloc[:, :] = nan；for i in range(result.shape[0]):<br>    temp_data = daily_ret[daily_ret.Trdmnt == result.index[i]]<br>    for j in range(result.shape[1]):<br>        temp = array(temp_data.iloc[:, j])<br>        not_nan_num = sum(~isnan(temp))<br>        if result.index[i][-2:] == '01' or result.index[i][-2:] == '02':<br>            if not_nan_num &gt;= min_feb_num:<br>                result.iloc[i, j] = nanmean(temp[argsort(temp)[:not_nan_num]][-mean_num:])<br>            else:<br>                result.iloc[i, j] = nan<br>        elif not_nan_num &gt;= min_day_num:<br>            result.iloc[i, j] = nanmean(temp[argsort(temp)[:not_nan_num]][-mean_num:])<br>        else:<br>            result.iloc[i, j] = nan；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1134)；全部来源：[A股特征 · mdr[mean_num=5, min_day_num=15, min_feb_num=5]（maximum daily return ）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1134) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原数学定义、原始科目映射、原样本过滤或完整计算/缺失回退规则尚未逐项核定；暂不列为可直接实现因子，不以简化近似冒充原项 |

2, j] + daily_ret.iloc[i - 1, j] + daily_ret.iloc[i, j] + daily_ret.iloc[i + 1, j]；result['Trdmnt'] = list(result.index)；result.Trdmnt = result.Trdmnt.apply(lambda x: x[0:6])；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1173)；全部来源：[A股特征 · abr（）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1173) | `f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：IAR_Rept-Annodt;TRD_Dalyr-Dretwd | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| season[year_num=5, min_year_num=3]（seasonality）；PF02004 | ret = monthly_ret.copy()；result = ret.copy()；result.iloc[:, :] = nan；for i in range(80, ret.shape[0]):<br>    for j in range(ret.shape[1]):<br>        temp = [ret.iloc[i - 11, j], ret.iloc[i - 23, j], ret.iloc[i - 35, j], ret.iloc[i - 47, j], ret.iloc[i - 59, j]]<br>        if sum(~isnan(temp)) &gt;= min_year_num:<br>            temp = nanmean(temp)<br>        else:<br>            temp = nan<br>        result.iloc[i, j] = temp；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1208)；全部来源：[A股特征 · season[year_num=5, min_year_num=3]（seasonality）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1208) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；原CSMAR依赖：股票月度收益率：TRD_Mnth-Mretwd | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：支持（字段设计；历史完整性未核验）；原美股交易所/样本筛选不自动复制到中证500 |
| roe（return on equity）；PF02005 | q_net_income = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B002000000')；oq_lag_bv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003000000').shift(3)；prefer = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003112101').shift(3)；roe = q_net_income / (oq_lag_bv - prefer)；输出=roe；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1236)；全部来源：[A股特征 · roe（return on equity）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1236) | `n_income`（`financial_income`）；`total_hldr_eqy_inc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B002000000;FS_Combas-A003000000；未对应原输入：[('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
