| rna（Return on Net Operating Assets）；PF02009 | op_in = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001300000')；cash = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001101000').shift(3)；short_inv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001109000').shift(3)；short_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002100000').shift(3)；long_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002206000').shift(3)；min_in = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003200000').shift(3)；prefer = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003112101').shift(3)；noa = short_debt + long_debt + min_in + prefer - cash - short_inv；rna = op_in / noa；输出=rna；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1268)；全部来源：[A股特征 · rna（Return on Net Operating Assets）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1268) | `operate_profit`（`financial_income`）；`money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`trad_asset`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`minority_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B001300000;FS_Combas-A001101000;FS_Combas-A001109000;FS_Combas-A002100000;FS_Combas-A002206000;FS_Combas-A003200000;FS_Combas-A003112101；未对应原输入：[('FS_Combas', 'A002206000'), ('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| ato（asset turnover）；PF02011 | op_rev = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001101000')；cash = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001101000').shift(3)；short_inv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001109000').shift(3)；short_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002100000').shift(3)；long_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002206000').shift(3)；min_in = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003200000').shift(3)；prefer = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003112101').shift(3)；noa = short_debt + long_debt + min_in + prefer - cash - short_inv；ato = op_rev / noa；输出=ato；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1287)；全部来源：[A股特征 · ato（asset turnover）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1287) | `revenue`（`financial_income`）；`money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`trad_asset`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`minority_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B001101000;FS_Combas-A001101000;FS_Combas-A001109000;FS_Combas-A002100000;FS_Combas-A002206000;FS_Combas-A003200000;FS_Combas-A003112101；未对应原输入：[('FS_Combas', 'A002206000'), ('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |

| Fscore（Fundamental Score）；PF02023 | q_net_income = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B002000000')；oq_lag_ta = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；froa = q_net_income / oq_lag_ta.shift(3)；for i in range(froa.shape[0]):<br>    for j in range(froa.shape[1]):<br>        if froa.iloc[i, j] &lt; 0:<br>            froa.iloc[i, j] = 0<br>        elif froa.iloc[i, j] &gt; 0:<br>            froa.iloc[i, j] = 1；op_cash_flow = get_data('FS_Comscfd', 'C001000000')；fcfa = op_cash_flow / oq_lag_ta.shift(3)；for i in range(fcfa.shape[0]):<br>    for j in range(fcfa.shape[1]):<br>        if fcfa.iloc[i, j] &lt;= 0:<br>            fcfa.iloc[i, j] = 0<br>        elif fcfa.iloc[i, j] &gt; 0:<br>            fcfa.iloc[i, j] = 1；fdroa = froa - froa.shift(12)；for i in range(fdroa.shape[0]):<br>    for j in range(fdroa.shape[1]):<br>        if fdroa.iloc[i, j] &lt;= 0:<br>            fdroa.iloc[i, j] = 0<br>        elif fdroa.iloc[i, j] &gt; 0:<br>            fdroa.iloc[i, j] = 1；long_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002206000')；lever = long_debt / ((oq_lag_ta + oq_lag_ta.shift(3)) / 2)；fdlever = lever - lever.shift(3)；for i in range(fdlever.shape[0]):<br>    for j in range(fdlever.shape[1]):<br>        if fdlever.iloc[i, j] &gt;= 0:<br>            fdlever.iloc[i, j] = 0<br>        elif fdlever.iloc[i, j] &lt; 0:<br>            fdlever.iloc[i, j] = 1；current_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001100000')；current_lia = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002100000')；current_ratio = current_assets / current_lia；fdliquid = current_ratio - current_ratio.shift(3)；for i in range(fdliquid.shape[0]):<br>    for j in range(fdliquid.shape[1]):<br>        if fdliquid.iloc[i, j] &lt;= 0:<br>            fdliquid.iloc[i, j] = 0<br>        elif fdliquid.iloc[i, j] &gt; 0:<br>            fdliquid.iloc[i, j] = 1；operating_profit = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001300000')；sales = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001101000')；margin = operating_profit / sales；fdmargin = margin - margin.shift(3)；for i in range(fdmargin.shape[0]):<br>    for j in range(fdmargin.shape[1]):<br>        if fdmargin.iloc[i, j] &lt;= 0:<br>            fdmargin.iloc[i, j] = 0<br>        elif fdmargin.iloc[i, j] &gt; 0:<br>            fdmargin.iloc[i, j] = 1；op_rev = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001101000')；dturn = op_rev / oq_lag_ta；fdturn = dturn - dturn.shift(3)；for i in range(fdturn.shape[0]):<br>    for j in range(fdturn.shape[1]):<br>        if fdturn.iloc[i, j] &lt;= 0:<br>            fdturn.iloc[i, j] = 0<br>        elif fdturn.iloc[i, j] &gt; 0:<br>            fdturn.iloc[i, j] = 1；Fscore = froa + fcfa + fdroa + fdlever + fdliquid + fdmargin + fdturn；输出=Fscore；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1394)；全部来源：[A股特征 · Fscore（Fundamental Score）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1394) | `n_income`（`financial_income`）；`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`n_cashflow_act`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`operate_profit`（`financial_income`）；`revenue`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B002000000;FS_Combas-A001000000;FS_Combas-C001000000;FS_Combas-A002206000;FS_Combas-A001100000;FS_Combas-A002100000;FS_Comins-B001300000;FS_Comins-B001101000;FS_Comins-B001101000；未对应原输入：[('FS_Combas', 'A002206000')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| Oscore（Ohlson O-score）；PF02024 | total_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；logta = total_assets.clip(lower=total_assets.quantile(0.01), upper=total_assets.quantile(0.99), axis=1)；total_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002000000')；tlta = total_assets / total_debt；tlta = tlta.clip(lower=tlta.quantile(0.01), upper=tlta.quantile(0.99), axis=1)；current_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001100000')；current_lia = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002100000')；wcta = (current_assets - current_lia) / total_assets；wcta = wcta.clip(lower=wcta.quantile(0.01), upper=wcta.quantile(0.99), axis=1)；clca = current_lia / current_assets；clca = clca.clip(lower=clca.quantile(0.01), upper=clca.quantile(0.99), axis=1)；oeneg = total_debt - total_assets；for i in range(oeneg.shape[0]):<br>    for j in range(oeneg.shape[1]):<br>        if oeneg.iloc[i, j] &lt;= 0:<br>            oeneg.iloc[i, j] = 0<br>        elif oeneg.iloc[i, j] &gt; 0:<br>            oeneg.iloc[i, j] = 1；net_profit = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B002000000')；nita = net_profit / total_assets；nita = nita.clip(lower=nita.quantile(0.01), upper=nita.quantile(0.99), axis=1)；earnings = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B002000000')；tax = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B002100000')；fund = earnings + tax；futl = fund / total_debt；futl = futl.clip(lower=futl.quantile(0.01), upper=futl.quantile(0.99), axis=1)；in2 = net_profit + net_profit.shift(3)；for i in range(in2.shape[0]):<br>    for j in range(in2.shape[1]):<br>        if in2.iloc[i, j] &gt;= 0:<br>            in2.iloc[i, j] = 0<br>        elif in2.iloc[i, j] &lt; 0:<br>            in2.iloc[i, j] = 1；chin = (net_profit - net_profit.shift(3)) / (abs(net_profit) + abs(net_profit.shift(3)))；Oscore = -1.32 - 0.407 * logta + 6.03 * tlta - 1.43 * wcta + 0.076 * clca - 1.72 * oeneg - 2.37 * nita - 1.83 * futl + 0.285 * in2 - 0.521 * chin；输出=Oscore；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1476)；全部来源：[A股特征 · Oscore（Ohlson O-score）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1476) | `total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`n_income`（`financial_income`）；`income_tax`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001000000;FS_Combas-A002000000;FS_Combas-A001100000;FS_Combas-A002100000;FS_Comins-B002000000;FS_Comins-B002000000;FS_Comins-B002100000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |

| sr（）；PF02030 | sales = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001101000', fs_freq='y')；sr = (sales.shift(48) - sales.shift(60)) / sales.shift(60)；for i in range(4, 0, -1):<br>    sr += (6 - i) * (sales.shift(i * 12 - 12) - sales.shift(i * 12)) / sales.shift(i * 12)；输出=sr；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1561)；全部来源：[A股特征 · sr（）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1561) | `revenue`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B001101000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| em（quarterly Enterprise Multiple ）；PF02031 | operating_revenue = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001101000')；market_equity = get_data('TRD_Mnth', 'Msmvosd')；market_equity = market_equity * 1000；tdebt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002000000')；prefer = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')；short_inv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001109000')；cash = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001101000')；enterprise_value = market_equity + tdebt + prefer - cash - short_inv；emq = enterprise_value / operating_revenue；输出=emq；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1569)；全部来源：[A股特征 · em（quarterly Enterprise Multiple ）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1569) | `revenue`（`financial_income`）；`circ_mv`（`stock_day`）；`total_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`trad_asset`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B001101000;TRD_Mnth-Msmvosd;FS_Combas-A002000000;FS_Combas-A001101000；未对应原输入：[('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| sp（Sales-to-Price Ratio）；PF02032 | operating_revenue = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001101000')；market_equity = get_data('TRD_Mnth', 'Msmvosd')；market_equity = market_equity * 1000；spq = operating_revenue / market_equity；输出=spq；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1583)；全部来源：[A股特征 · sp（Sales-to-Price Ratio）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1583) | `revenue`（`financial_income`）；`circ_mv`（`stock_day`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B001101000;TRD_Mnth-Msmvosd | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| ocfp（operating cash flow to price ratio）；PF02033 | op_cash_flow = get_data('FS_Comscfd', 'C001000000')；market_equity = get_data('TRD_Mnth', 'Msmvosd')；market_equity = market_equity * 1000；ocfpq = op_cash_flow / market_equity；输出=ocfpq；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1591)；全部来源：[A股特征 · ocfp（operating cash flow to price ratio）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1591) | `n_cashflow_act`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`circ_mv`（`stock_day`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：CFO：FS_Comscfd-C001000000；市值：TRD_Mnth-Msmvosd | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| de（Liabilities-to-Book Equity）；PF02034 | total_liabilities = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002000000', fs_freq='y')；total_shareholder_equity = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003000000', fs_freq='y')；prefer = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')；de = total_liabilities / (total_shareholder_equity - prefer)；输出=de；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1599)；全部来源：[A股特征 · de（Liabilities-to-Book Equity）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1599) | `total_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_hldr_eqy_inc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A002000000;FS_Combas-A003000000；未对应原输入：[('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| ebp（Quarterly Enterprise Book-to-Price）；PF02035 | total_shareholder_equity = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003000000')；prefer = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')；book_equity = total_shareholder_equity - prefer；market_equity = get_data('TRD_Mnth', 'Msmvosd')；market_equity = market_equity * 1000；long_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002206000')；short_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002100000')；short_inv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001109000')；cash = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001101000')；net_debt = long_debt + short_debt + prefer - cash - short_inv；ebp = (net_debt + book_equity) / (net_debt + market_equity)；输出=ebp；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1608)；全部来源：[A股特征 · ebp（Quarterly Enterprise Book-to-Price）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1608) | `total_hldr_eqy_inc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`circ_mv`（`stock_day`）；`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`trad_asset`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A003000000;FS_Combas-A003112101;TRD_Mnth-Msmvosd;FS_Combas-A002206000;FS_Combas-A002100000;FS_Combas-A001109000;FS_Combas-A001101000；未对应原输入：[('FS_Combas', 'A003112101'), ('FS_Combas', 'A002206000')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| ndp（Quarterly Net Debt-to-Price）；PF02036 | total_shareholder_equity = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003000000')；prefer = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')；book_equity = total_shareholder_equity - prefer；market_equity = get_data('TRD_Mnth', 'Msmvosd')；market_equity = market_equity * 1000；long_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002206000')；short_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002100000')；short_inv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001109000')；cash = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001101000')；net_debt = long_debt + short_debt + prefer - cash - short_inv；ndp = net_debt / market_equity；输出=ndp；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1624)；全部来源：[A股特征 · ndp（Quarterly Net Debt-to-Price）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1624) | `total_hldr_eqy_inc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`circ_mv`（`stock_day`）；`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`trad_asset`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A003000000;FS_Combas-A003112101;TRD_Mnth-Msmvosd;FS_Combas-A002206000;FS_Combas-A002100000;FS_Combas-A001109000;FS_Combas-A001101000；未对应原输入：[('FS_Combas', 'A003112101'), ('FS_Combas', 'A002206000')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| dpia（Changes in PPE and inventory-to-assets）；PF02038 | fixed_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001212000')；inventory = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001123000')；total_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000').shift(12)；dpia = (fixed_assets + inventory) / total_assets；输出=dpia；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1650)；全部来源：[A股特征 · dpia（Changes in PPE and inventory-to-assets）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1650) | `fix_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`inventories`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001212000;FS_Combas-A001123000;FS_Combas-A001000000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| noa（Net Operating Assets）；PF02039 | total_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000').shift(12)；cash = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001101000')；short_inv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001109000')；short_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002100000')；long_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002206000')；min_in = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003200000')；prefer = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')；noa = short_debt + long_debt + min_in + prefer - cash - short_inv；noa = noa / total_assets；输出=noa；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1659)；全部来源：[A股特征 · noa（Net Operating Assets）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1659) | `money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`trad_asset`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`minority_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001000000;FS_Combas-A001101000;FS_Combas-A001109000;FS_Combas-A002100000;FS_Combas-A002206000;FS_Combas-A003200000;FS_Combas-A003112101；未对应原输入：[('FS_Combas', 'A002206000'), ('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| dnoa（Changes in Net Operating Assets）；PF02040 | total_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000').shift(12)；cash = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001101000')；short_inv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001109000')；short_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002100000')；long_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002206000')；min_in = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003200000')；prefer = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')；noa = short_debt + long_debt + min_in + prefer - cash - short_inv；result = (noa.shift(12) - noa) / total_assets；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1672)；全部来源：[A股特征 · dnoa（Changes in Net Operating Assets）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1672) | `money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`trad_asset`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`minority_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001000000;FS_Combas-A001101000;FS_Combas-A001109000;FS_Combas-A002100000;FS_Combas-A002206000;FS_Combas-A003200000;FS_Combas-A003112101；未对应原输入：[('FS_Combas', 'A002206000'), ('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| ig（1-year Investment Growth）；PF02041 | cap_exp = get_data('FS_Comscfd', 'C002006000')；ig = (cap_exp - cap_exp.shift(12)) / cap_exp.shift(12)；输出=ig；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1687)；全部来源：[A股特征 · ig（1-year Investment Growth）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1687) | `c_pay_acq_const_fiolta`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comscfd-C002006000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| cei（Composite Equity Issuance）；PF02042 | market_equity = get_data('TRD_Mnth', 'Msmvosd')；market_equity = market_equity * 1000；ret = monthly_ret.copy()；result = 1；for i in range(1, 12 + 1):<br>    temp_lag = ret.shift(i)<br>    result = result * (1 + temp_lag)；result = result - 1；输出=cei；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1693)；全部来源：[A股特征 · cei（Composite Equity Issuance）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1693) | `circ_mv`（`stock_day`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：TRD_Mnth-Msmvosd | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原数学定义、原始科目映射、原样本过滤或完整计算/缺失回退规则尚未逐项核定；暂不列为可直接实现因子，不以简化近似冒充原项 |
| cdi（Composite Debt Issuance）；PF02043 | total_liabilities = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002000000')；输出=cdi；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1709)；全部来源：[A股特征 · cdi（Composite Debt Issuance）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1709) | `total_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A002000000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| ivchg（Inventory Change）；PF02045 | total_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；inventory = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001123000')；ivchg = (inventory - inventory.shift(12)) / total_assets.shift(12)；输出=ivchg；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1721)；全部来源：[A股特征 · ivchg（Inventory Change）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1721) | `total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`inventories`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001000000;FS_Combas-A001123000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| oacc（Operating Accruals）；PF02046 | total_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；net_profit = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B002000000')；op_cash_flows = get_data('FS_Comscfd', 'C001000000')；oacc = (net_profit - op_cash_flows) / total_assets.shift(12)；输出=oacc；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1728)；全部来源：[A股特征 · oacc（Operating Accruals）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1728) | `total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`n_income`（`financial_income`）；`n_cashflow_act`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001000000;FS_Comins-B002000000;FS_Comscfd-C001000000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| acc（Total Accruals）；PF02047 | t_profit = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001000000')；cash_flows = get_data('FS_Comscfd', 'C005000000')；total_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；acc = (t_profit - cash_flows) / total_assets.shift(12)；输出=acc；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1736)；全部来源：[A股特征 · acc（Total Accruals）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1736) | `total_profit`（`financial_income`）；`n_incr_cash_cash_equ`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B001000000;FS_Comscfd-C001000000;FS_Combas-A001000000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| dwc（Changes in Net Noncash Working Capital）；PF02048 | total_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；current_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001100000')；cash = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001101000')；short_inv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001109000')；coa = current_assets - cash - short_inv；current_lia = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002100000')；short_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002101000')；col = current_lia - short_debt；wc = coa - col；dwc = (wc - wc.shift(12)) / total_assets.shift(12)；输出=dwc；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1744)；全部来源：[A股特征 · dwc（Changes in Net Noncash Working Capital）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1744) | `total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`trad_asset`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`st_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001000000;FS_Combas-A001100000;FS_Combas-A001101000;FS_Combas-A001109000;FS_Combas-A002100000;FS_Combas-A002101000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| dcoa（change in Current Operating Assets）；PF02049 | total_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；current_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001100000')；cash = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001101000')；short_inv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001109000')；coa = current_assets - cash - short_inv；dcoa = (coa - coa.shift(12)) / total_assets.shift(12)；输出=dcoa；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1758)；全部来源：[A股特征 · dcoa（change in Current Operating Assets）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1758) | `total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`trad_asset`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001000000;FS_Combas-A001100000;FS_Combas-A001101000;FS_Combas-A001109000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |

| dnco（Changes in Net Noncurrent Operating Assets）；PF02051 | total_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；current_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001100000')；long_inv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001207000')；nca = total_assets - current_assets - long_inv；total_liabilities = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002000000')；current_lia = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002100000')；long_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002201000')；ncl = total_liabilities - current_lia - long_debt；nco = nca - ncl；dnco = (nco - nco.shift(12)) / total_assets.shift(12)；输出=dnco；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1777)；全部来源：[A股特征 · dnco（Changes in Net Noncurrent Operating Assets）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1777) | `total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`lt_eqt_invest`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`lt_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001000000;FS_Combas-A001100000;FS_Combas-A001207000;FS_Combas-A002000000;FS_Combas-A002100000;FS_Combas-A002201000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |

| dncl（change in Noncurrent Operating Liabilities）；PF02053 | total_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；total_liabilities = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002000000')；current_lia = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002100000')；long_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002201000')；ncl = total_liabilities - current_lia - long_debt；dncl = (ncl - ncl.shift(12)) / total_assets.shift(12)；输出=dncl；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1799)；全部来源：[A股特征 · dncl（change in Noncurrent Operating Liabilities）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1799) | `total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`lt_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001000000;FS_Combas-A002000000;FS_Combas-A002100000;FS_Combas-A002201000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| dfin（Changes in Net Financial Assets）；PF02054 | total_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；short_inv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001109000')；long_inv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001207000')；financial_assets = short_inv + long_inv；short_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002101000')；long_debt = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A002201000')；prefer = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')；financial_lia = short_debt + long_debt + prefer；fin = financial_assets - financial_lia；dfin = (fin - fin.shift(12)) / total_assets.shift(12)；输出=dfin；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1811)；全部来源：[A股特征 · dfin（Changes in Net Financial Assets）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1811) | `total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`trad_asset`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`lt_eqt_invest`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`st_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`lt_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001000000;FS_Combas-A001109000;FS_Combas-A001207000;FS_Combas-A002101000;FS_Combas-A002201000;FS_Combas-A003112101；未对应原输入：[('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| dbe（change in Book Equity）；PF02055 | total_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；total_shareholder_equity = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003000000')；dbe = (total_shareholder_equity - total_shareholder_equity.shift(12)) / total_assets.shift(12)；输出=dbe；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1826)；全部来源：[A股特征 · dbe（change in Book Equity）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1826) | `total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_hldr_eqy_inc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001000000;FS_Combas-A003000000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| adm（Advertising Expense-to-Market）；PF02056 | sell_exp = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001209000')；market_equity = get_data('TRD_Mnth', 'Msmvosd')；market_equity = market_equity * 1000；adm = sell_exp / market_equity；输出=adm；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1835)；全部来源：[A股特征 · adm（Advertising Expense-to-Market）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1835) | `sell_exp`（`financial_income`）；`circ_mv`（`stock_day`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B001209000;TRD_Mnth-Msmvosd | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果）；原项目使用管理/销售费用代理研发/广告，不能视为真实研发/广告定义；本框架rd_exp是真实研发候选，替换后须另命名 |
| gad（Growth in Advertising Expense）；PF02057 | sell_exp = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001209000')；gad = (sell_exp - sell_exp.shift(12)) / sell_exp.shift(12)；输出=gad；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1843)；全部来源：[A股特征 · gad（Growth in Advertising Expense）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1843) | `sell_exp`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B001209000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果）；原项目使用管理/销售费用代理研发/广告，不能视为真实研发/广告定义；本框架rd_exp是真实研发候选，替换后须另命名 |
| rdm（R&amp;D Expense to Market Equity）；PF02058 | management_fee = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001210000')；market_equity = get_data('TRD_Mnth', 'Msmvosd')；market_equity = market_equity * 1000；rdm = management_fee / market_equity；输出=rdm；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1849)；全部来源：[A股特征 · rdm（R&amp;D Expense to Market Equity）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1849) | `admin_exp`（`financial_income`）；`circ_mv`（`stock_day`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B001210000;TRD_Mnth-Msmvosd | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果）；原项目使用管理/销售费用代理研发/广告，不能视为真实研发/广告定义；本框架rd_exp是真实研发候选，替换后须另命名 |
| rds（R&amp;D Expense to Sales Ratio）；PF02059 | management_fee = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001210000')；sales = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001101000')；rds = management_fee / sales；输出=rds；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1858)；全部来源：[A股特征 · rds（R&amp;D Expense to Sales Ratio）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1858) | `admin_exp`（`financial_income`）；`revenue`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B001210000;FS_Comins-B001101000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果）；原项目使用管理/销售费用代理研发/广告，不能视为真实研发/广告定义；本框架rd_exp是真实研发候选，替换后须另命名 |
| hn（Hiring Rate）；PF02061 | mon_ret = monthly_ret.copy()；mon_ret.iloc[:, :] = 1；employee = get_data('CG_Ybasic', 'Y0601b')；employee = mon_ret * employee；hn = (employee - employee.shift(12)) / (0.5 * employee + 0.5 * employee.shift(12))；hn.index.names = ['Trdmnt']；输出=hn；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1873)；全部来源：[A股特征 · hn（Hiring Rate）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1873) | `industry_sw_1`（`stock_day`）；`industry_sw_2`（`stock_day`）；`industry_sw_3`（`stock_day`）；原CSMAR依赖：CG_Ybasic-Y0601b；未对应原输入：[('CG_Ybasic', 'Y0601b')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| age（Firm Age）；PF02062 | listdate = get_data('CG_Co', 'ListedDate')；result = monthly_ret.copy()；result.iloc[:, :] = nan；for i in listdate.index:<br>    if i not in result.iloc[1, :].index:<br>        listdate.drop(index=i, inplace=True)；for i in range(result.shape[0]):<br>    for j in range(result.shape[1]):<br>        time = (int(result.index[i][0:4]) - int(listdate.iloc[j, 0][0:4])) * 12 + int(result.index[i][4:6]) - int(listdate.iloc[j, 0][4:6])<br>        if time &gt;= 0:<br>            result.iloc[i, j] = time<br>        else:<br>            result.iloc[i, j] = nan；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1884)；全部来源：[A股特征 · age（Firm Age）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1884) | 所需原始数据：Accounting；文档未提供可验证的完整映射；缺失：成立/上市/首次覆盖日期，最新版未列；不能用样本首日代替；原CSMAR依赖：CG_Co-ListedDate；未对应原输入：[('CG_Co', 'ListedDate')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| dsa（）；PF02064 | sales = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001101000')；apc_sales = (sales - sales.shift(12)) / sales.shift(12)；acc_rec = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001111000')；apc_acc_rec = (acc_rec - acc_rec.shift(12)) / acc_rec.shift(12)；dsa = apc_sales - apc_acc_rec；输出=dsa；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1911)；全部来源：[A股特征 · dsa（）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1911) | `revenue`（`financial_income`）；`accounts_receiv`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B001101000;FS_Combas-A001111000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| dss（% Change in Sales minus % Change in SG&amp;A）；PF02066 | sales = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001101000')；apc_sales = (sales - sales.shift(12)) / sales.shift(12)；sell_exp = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001209000')；admin_exp = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001210000')；SGA = sell_exp + admin_exp；apc_SGA = (SGA - SGA.shift(12)) / SGA.shift(12)；dss = apc_sales - apc_SGA；输出=dss；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1929)；全部来源：[A股特征 · dss（% Change in Sales minus % Change in SG&amp;A）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1929) | `revenue`（`financial_income`）；`sell_exp`（`financial_income`）；`admin_exp`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B001101000;FS_Comins-B001209000;FS_Comins-B001210000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| etr（Effective Tax Rate）；PF02067 | tax_exp = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B002100000')；earnings = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B002000000')；tax = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B002100000')；EBT = earnings + tax；net_profit = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B002000000')；market_equity = get_data('TRD_Mnth', 'Msmvosd')；market_equity = market_equity * 1000；eps = net_profit / market_equity；deps = eps - eps.shift(12)；te = tax_exp / EBT；etr = (te - (te.shift(36) + te.shift(24) + te.shift(12)) / 3) * deps；输出=etr；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1940)；全部来源：[A股特征 · etr（Effective Tax Rate）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1940) | `income_tax`（`financial_income`）；`n_income`（`financial_income`）；`circ_mv`（`stock_day`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B002100000;FS_Comins-B002000000;FS_Comins-B002100000;FS_Comins-B002000000;TRD_Mnth-Msmvosd | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| lfe（Effective Tax Rate）；PF02068 | sales = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B001101000')；mon_ret = monthly_ret.copy()；mon_ret.iloc[:, :] = 1；employee = get_data('CG_Ybasic', 'Y0601b')；employee = mon_ret * employee；lfe = (sales / employee - sales.shift(12) / employee.shift(12)) / (sales.shift(12) / employee.shift(12))；lfe.index.names = ['Trdmnt']；输出=lfe；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1955)；全部来源：[A股特征 · lfe（Effective Tax Rate）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1955) | `revenue`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B001101000;CG_Ybasic-Y0601b；未对应原输入：[('CG_Ybasic', 'Y0601b')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| tan（Quarterly Tangibility）；PF02069 | cash = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001101000')；short_inv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001109000')；acc_rec = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001111000')；inventory = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001123000')；fixed_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001212000')；ta = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；tan = cash + short_inv + 0.715 * acc_rec + 0.547 * inventory + 0.535 * fixed_assets；tan = tan / ta；输出=tan；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1968)；全部来源：[A股特征 · tan（Quarterly Tangibility）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1968) | `money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`trad_asset`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`accounts_receiv`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`inventories`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`fix_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001101000;FS_Combas-A001109000;FS_Combas-A001111000;FS_Combas-A001123000;FS_Combas-A001212000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| vcf[month_num=60, min_month_num=20]（Cash Flow Volatility）；PF02070 | cash_flows = get_data('FS_Comscfd', 'C005000000')；result = cash_flows.rolling(month_num, min_periods=min_month_num).apply(lambda x: std(x) / mean(x) if mean(x) != 0 else nan)；输出=result；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1981)；全部来源：[A股特征 · vcf[month_num=60, min_month_num=20]（Cash Flow Volatility）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1981) | `n_incr_cash_cash_equ`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comscfd-C005000000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| cta（Cash to Assets）；PF02071 | ccq = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001101000')；ta = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；cta = ccq / ta；输出=cta；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1988)；全部来源：[A股特征 · cta（Cash to Assets）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1988) | `money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001101000;FS_Combas-A001000000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| esm[month_num=36, min_month_num=12]（Earnings Smoothness）；PF02072 | earnings = get_data('FS_Comins', 'B002000000')；total_asset = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；oper_cash_flow = get_data('FS_Comscfd', 'C001000000')；esm = (earnings / total_asset.shift(12)).rolling(month_num, min_periods=min_month_num).std() / (oper_cash_flow / total_asset.shift(12)).rolling(month_num, min_periods=min_month_num).std()；输出=esm；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1995)；全部来源：[A股特征 · esm[month_num=36, min_month_num=12]（Earnings Smoothness）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L1995) | `n_income`（`financial_income`）；`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`n_cashflow_act`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Comins-B002000000;FS_Combas-A001000000;FS_Comscfd-C001000000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| ala（Asset Liquidity (annual)）；PF02073 | cash = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001101000')；current_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001100000')；short_inv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001109000')；total_asset = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；goodwill = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001220000')；intangibles = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001218000')；al = cash + 0.75 * (current_assets - cash) + 0.5 * (total_asset - current_assets - goodwill - intangibles)；ala = al / total_asset.shift(3)；输出=ala；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L2004)；全部来源：[A股特征 · ala（Asset Liquidity (annual)）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L2004) | `money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`trad_asset`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`goodwill`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`intan_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001101000;FS_Combas-A001100000;FS_Combas-A001109000;FS_Combas-A001000000;FS_Combas-A001220000;FS_Combas-A001218000 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| alm（Asset Liquidity (monthly)）；PF02074 | cash = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001101000')；current_assets = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001100000')；short_inv = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001109000')；total_asset = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001000000')；goodwill = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001220000')；intangibles = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A001218000')；al = cash + 0.75 * (current_assets - cash) + 0.5 * (total_asset - current_assets - goodwill - intangibles)；market_equity = get_data('TRD_Mnth', 'Msmvosd')；market_equity = market_equity * 1000；total_eq = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003000000')；prefer = get_data('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')；book_eq = total_eq - prefer；alm = al / (total_asset + market_equity - book_eq).shift(3)；输出=alm；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L2015)；全部来源：[A股特征 · alm（Asset Liquidity (monthly)）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L2015) | `money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`trad_asset`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`goodwill`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`intan_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`circ_mv`（`stock_day`）；`total_hldr_eqy_inc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：FS_Combas-A001101000;FS_Combas-A001100000;FS_Combas-A001109000;FS_Combas-A001000000;FS_Combas-A001220000;FS_Combas-A001218000;TRD_Mnth-Msmvosd;FS_Combas-A003000000;FS_Combas-A003112101；未对应原输入：[('FS_Combas', 'A003112101')] | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：部分/不支持原定义（仍有未对应输入） |
| sue[change_qnum=4, std_qnum=8, std_min_qnum=6]（standardized unexpected earnings）；PF02075 | raw_data = read_csv(local_data_path + 'FS_Comins.csv')；raw_data['type'] = raw_data.Accper.apply(lambda x: x[5:7] + x[8:10])；raw_data.Accper = raw_data.Accper.apply(lambda x: x[0:4] + x[5:7])；raw_data = raw_data.pivot(index='Accper', columns='Stkcd', values='B002000000')；earnings = raw_data.copy()；for i in range(1, earnings.shape[0]):<br>    if earnings.index[i - 1][-2:] != '12':<br>        earnings.iloc[i, :] = raw_data.iloc[i, :] - raw_data.iloc[i - 1, :]；earnings_lag = earnings.shift(change_qnum)；change = earnings - earnings_lag；std = earnings.copy()；std.iloc[:, :] = nan；for i in range(earnings.shape[0]):<br>    for j in range(earnings.shape[1]):<br>        temp = array(earnings.iloc[i - (std_qnum - 1):i + 1, j])<br>        not_nan_num = sum(~isnan(temp))<br>        if not_nan_num &gt;= std_min_qnum:<br>            temp = temp[~isnan(temp)]<br>            temp = diff(temp)<br>            std.iloc[i, j] = std(temp, ddof=1)；result = change / std；new_ind = []；for i in range(result.shape[0]):<br>    temp = result.index[i]<br>    if temp[-2:] == '12':<br>        temp = str(int(temp[0:4]) + 1) + '05'<br>    elif temp[-2:] == '03':<br>        temp = temp[0:4] + '07'<br>    elif temp[-2:] == '06':<br>        temp = temp[0:4] + '10'<br>    elif temp[-2:] == '09':<br>        temp = str(int(temp[0:4]) + 1) + '01'<br>    new_ind.append(temp)；result['new_ind'] = new_ind；universe = monthly_ret.copy()；data = universe.copy()；data.iloc[:, :] = nan；data.loc[:, :] = result.loc[:, :]；universe = isnan(universe)；data[universe] = nan；输出=data；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L2033)；全部来源：[A股特征 · sue[change_qnum=4, std_qnum=8, std_min_qnum=6]（standardized unexpected earnings）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L2033) | `n_income`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：all data | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |

1][-2:] != '12':<br>        earnings.iloc[i, :] = raw_data.iloc[i, :] - raw_data.iloc[i - 1, :]；earnings_lag = earnings.shift(change_qnum)；change = earnings - earnings_lag；std = earnings.copy()；std.iloc[:, :] = nan；for i in range(earnings.shape[0]):<br>    for j in range(earnings.shape[1]):<br>        temp = array(earnings.iloc[i - (std_qnum - 1):i + 1, j])<br>        not_nan_num = sum(~isnan(temp))<br>        if not_nan_num &gt;= std_min_qnum:<br>            temp = temp[~isnan(temp)]<br>            temp = diff(temp)<br>            std.iloc[i, j] = std(temp, ddof=1)；result = change / std；new_ind = []；for i in range(result.shape[0]):<br>    temp = result.index[i]<br>    if temp[-2:] == '12':<br>        temp = str(int(temp[0:4]) + 1) + '05'<br>    elif temp[-2:] == '03':<br>        temp = temp[0:4] + '07'<br>    elif temp[-2:] == '06':<br>        temp = temp[0:4] + '10'<br>    elif temp[-2:] == '09':<br>        temp = str(int(temp[0:4]) + 1) + '01'<br>    new_ind.append(temp)；result['new_ind'] = new_ind；universe = monthly_ret.copy()；data = universe.copy()；data.iloc[:, :] = nan；data.loc[:, :] = result.loc[:, :]；universe = isnan(universe)；data[universe] = nan；输出=data；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L2086)；全部来源：[A股特征 · rs[change_qnum=4, std_qnum=8, std_min_qnum=6]（revenue surprise）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L2086) | `total_revenue`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：all data | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果） |
| tes[change_qnum=4, std_qnum=8, std_min_qnum=6]（tax expense surprise）；PF02077 | raw_data = read_csv(local_data_path + 'FS_Comins.csv')；raw_data['type'] = raw_data.Accper.apply(lambda x: x[5:7] + x[8:10])；raw_data.Accper = raw_data.Accper.apply(lambda x: x[0:4] + x[5:7])；raw_data = raw_data.pivot(index='Accper', columns='Stkcd', values='B001100000')；earnings = raw_data.copy()；for i in range(1, earnings.shape[0]):<br>    if earnings.index[i - 1][-2:] != '12':<br>        earnings.iloc[i, :] = raw_data.iloc[i, :] - raw_data.iloc[i - 1, :]；earnings_lag = earnings.shift(change_qnum)；result = (earnings - earnings_lag) / earnings_lag；new_ind = []；for i in range(result.shape[0]):<br>    temp = result.index[i]<br>    if temp[-2:] == '12':<br>        temp = str(int(temp[0:4]) + 1) + '05'<br>    elif temp[-2:] == '03':<br>        temp = temp[0:4] + '07'<br>    elif temp[-2:] == '06':<br>        temp = temp[0:4] + '10'<br>    elif temp[-2:] == '09':<br>        temp = str(int(temp[0:4]) + 1) + '01'<br>    new_ind.append(temp)；result['new_ind'] = new_ind；universe = monthly_ret.copy()；data = universe.copy()；data.iloc[:, :] = nan；data.loc[:, :] = result.loc[:, :]；universe = isnan(universe)；data[universe] = nan；输出=data；[原代码/规则](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L2139)；全部来源：[A股特征 · tes[change_qnum=4, std_qnum=8, std_min_qnum=6]（tax expense surprise）](https://github.com/Quantactix/ChinaAShareEquityCharacteristics/blob/ed1935598f8ae9ec4588fbe066e5e08d683c652c/code/main.py#L2139) | `total_revenue`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；上述CSMAR→Tushare为候选经济科目映射，单位/口径仍需核对；原依赖：all data | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（A股科目适配；按公告日PIT，累计值差分得到单季；不等同原美股结果）；原代码实际pivot B001100000（营业收入），与税费惊喜名称冲突，不能认定税费原定义已支持 |

| FUNDA.eq_dur；PF02157 | T = 10；r = 0.12；roe_mean = 0.12；roe_b1 = 0.57；roe = ib / where(lag(be) &gt; 1, lag(be), nan)；roe_c = roe_mean * (1 - roe_b1)；g_mean = 0.06；g_b1 = 0.24；g = where(lag(sale) &gt; 1, growth(sale), nan)；g_c = g_mean * (1 - g_b1)；be = be；ed_cdw = 0；ed_cd = 0；ed_err = zeros(be.shape)；for t in range(1, T + 1):<br>    roe, g = (roe_c + roe_b1 * roe, g_c + g_b1 * g)<br>    be, cd = (be * (1 + g), be * (roe - g))<br>    ed_cdw = ed_cdw + t * cd / (1 + r) ^ t<br>    ed_cd = ed_cd + cd / (1 + r) ^ t<br>    ed_err = where(be &lt; 0, 1, ed_err)；edc = T + 1 + 1 / r；eq_dur = ed_cdw / me + edc * (me - ed_cd) / me；eq_dur = where((ed_err == 1) &#124; (eq_dur &lt;= 1), nan, eq_dur)；输出=eq_dur；[确切算法与预处理](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L991)；全部来源：[PyAnomaly · FUNDA.eq_dur](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L991) | ceq→`total_hldr_eqy_exc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；txditc→`defer_tax_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；sale→`revenue`（`financial_income`）；ib→`n_income`（`financial_income`）；`close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；`total_mv`（`stock_day`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；缺失：优先股账面价值、公司治理指数 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：不支持原定义（缺上述输入）；已有字段只能形成另行命名的简化/适配版本 |
| FUNDA.f_score；PF02158 | at_1 = lag(at_, 1)；f_roa = ib / at_1；f_croa = ocf / at_1；f_droa = diff(f_roa)；f_acc = f_croa - f_roa；f_liq = diff(act / lct)；f_gm = diff(gp / sale)；f_aturn = diff(sale / at_1)；if config.replicate_jkp:<br>    f_lev = diff(dltt / at_)<br>    f_eqis = sstk<br>else:<br>    f_lev = diff(dltt / avgat)<br>    f_eqis = (sstk - pstk).fillna(0)；char = (f_roa &gt; ZERO).astype(int) + (f_croa &gt; ZERO).astype(int) + (f_droa &gt; ZERO).astype(int) + (f_acc &gt; ZERO).astype(int) + (f_lev &lt; ZERO).astype(int) + (f_liq &gt; ZERO).astype(int) + is_zero(f_eqis).astype(int) + (f_gm &gt; ZERO).astype(int) + (f_aturn &gt; ZERO).astype(int)；输出=char.astype(config.float_type)；[确切算法与预处理](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1026)；全部来源：[PyAnomaly · FUNDA.f_score](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1026) | ROA→`roa`（`financial_indicator`）；at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；act→`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lct→`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dltt→`lt_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；sale→`revenue`（`financial_income`）；cogs→`oper_cost`（`financial_income`）；ib→`n_income`（`financial_income`）；oancf→`n_cashflow_act`（`financial_cash_flow`）；sstk→`c_recp_cap_contrib`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_indicator`）；`end_date`（`financial_indicator`）；缺失：优先股账面价值 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：不支持原定义（缺上述输入）；已有字段只能形成另行命名的简化/适配版本 |
| FUNDA.ps；PF02159 | act = act.fillna(che + rect + invt)；lct = lct.fillna(ap)；char = (ib &gt; 0).astype(int) + (oancf &gt; 0).astype(int) + (diff(ib / at_) &gt; 0).astype(int) + (oancf &gt; ib).astype(int) + (diff(dltt / at_) &lt; 0).astype(int) + (diff(act / lct) &gt; 0).astype(int) + (diff(sale - cogs) / sale &gt; 0).astype(int) + (diff(sale / at_) &gt; 0).astype(int) + (scstkc == 0).astype(int)；输出=char.astype(config.float_type)；[确切算法与预处理](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1052)；全部来源：[PyAnomaly · FUNDA.ps](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1052) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；act→`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lct→`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；che→`money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；invt→`inventories`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；rect→`accounts_receiv`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ap→`acct_payable`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dltt→`lt_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；sale→`revenue`（`financial_income`）；cogs→`oper_cost`（`financial_income`）；ib→`n_income`（`financial_income`）；oancf→`n_cashflow_act`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`） | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；原数据库到A股物理字段仍有实质科目/代理差异，不能直接宣称原定义可复现：条件支持（PIT与中国科目/样本适配）；原定义含短期投资，不只有money_cap；原长期债务范围比lt_borr宽，需加债券等；原非常项目前利润不等同中国净利润 |
| FUNDA.o_score；PF02160 | at = at_.copy()；nix = nix；nix_1 = lag(nix)；o_lev = debt / at；o_wc = (act - lct) / at；o_roe = nix / at；o_cacl = lct / act；o_cacl[act &lt; ZERO] = nan；o_ffo = (pi + dp) / lt_；o_ffo[lt_ &lt; ZERO] = nan；o_neg_eq = lt_ &gt; at_；o_neg_earn = (nix &lt; 0) &amp; (nix_1 &lt; 0)；denom = abs(nix) + abs(nix_1)；o_nich = (nix - nix_1) / denom；o_nich[denom &lt; ZERO] = nan；char = -1.32 - 0.407 * o_lat + 6.03 * o_lev + 1.43 * o_wc + 0.076 * o_cacl - 1.72 * o_neg_eq - 2.37 * o_roe - 1.83 * o_ffo + 0.285 * o_neg_earn - 0.52 * o_nich；输出=char；[确切算法与预处理](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1069)；全部来源：[PyAnomaly · FUNDA.o_score](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1069) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；act→`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lt→`total_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lct→`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dltt→`lt_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dlc→`st_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dp→`depr_fa_coga_dpba`（`financial_cash_flow`）；pi→`total_profit`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；缺失：优先股账面价值 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：不支持原定义（缺上述输入）；已有字段只能形成另行命名的简化/适配版本 |
| FUNDA.z_score；PF02161 | z_wc = (act - lct) / at_；z_re = re / at_；z_eb = ebitda / at_；z_sa = sale / at_；z_me = me_fiscal / lt_；z_me[lt_ &lt;= 0] = nan；char = 1.2 * z_wc + 1.4 * z_re + 3.3 * z_eb + 0.6 * z_me + 1.0 * z_sa；输出=char；[确切算法与预处理](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1098)；全部来源：[PyAnomaly · FUNDA.z_score](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1098) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；act→`total_cur_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lt→`total_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；lct→`total_cur_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；sale→`revenue`（`financial_income`）；ebitda→`ebitda`（`financial_income`）；re→`retained_earnings`（`financial_indicator`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；`ann_date`（`financial_indicator`）；`end_date`（`financial_indicator`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原数学定义、原始科目映射、原样本过滤或完整计算/缺失回退规则尚未逐项核定；暂不列为可直接实现因子，不以简化近似冒充原项 |
| FUNDA.kz_index；PF02162 | ppent_1 = lag(ppent)；ppent_1[ppent_1 &lt;= 0] = nan；kz_cf = (ib + dp) / ppent_1；if config.replicate_jkp:<br>    kz_q = (at_ + me_fiscal - be) / at_<br>else:<br>    kz_q = (at_ + me_fiscal - be - txdb) / at_；kz_db = debt / (debt + seq)；kz_db[debt + seq == 0] = nan；kz_dv = dvt / ppent_1；kz_cs = che / ppent_1；char = -1.002 * kz_cf + 0.283 * kz_q + 3.139 * kz_db - 39.368 * kz_dv - 1.315 * kz_cs；输出=char；[确切算法与预处理](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1114)；全部来源：[PyAnomaly · FUNDA.kz_index](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1114) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ceq→`total_hldr_eqy_exc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；che→`money_cap`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ppent→`fix_assets_total`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dltt→`lt_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；dlc→`st_borr`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；txditc→`defer_tax_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ib→`n_income`（`financial_income`）；dp→`depr_fa_coga_dpba`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`end_date`（`financial_cash_flow`）；`ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_income`）；`end_date`（`financial_income`）；缺失：优先股账面价值、总股息原口径 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：不支持原定义（缺上述输入）；已有字段只能形成另行命名的简化/适配版本 |
| FUNDA.intrinsic_value；PF02163 | r = 0.12；k = dvt / nix；k[nix &lt;= 0] = dvt / 0.06 / at_；be = rolling(be, 2, 'mean')；be[be &lt;= 0] = nan；Eroe = nix / be；be1 = (1 + (1 - k) * Eroe) * be；char = be + (Eroe - r) / (1 + r) * be + (Eroe - r) / r / (1 + r) * be1；char[char &lt;= 0] = nan；输出=char；[确切算法与预处理](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1136)；全部来源：[PyAnomaly · FUNDA.intrinsic_value](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L1136) | at→`total_assets`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；ceq→`total_hldr_eqy_exc_min_int`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；txditc→`defer_tax_liab`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`industry_sw_1`（`stock_day`）；`industry_sw_2`（`stock_day`）；`industry_sw_3`（`stock_day`）；`ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`f_ann_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；`end_date`（`financial_balance_sheet`）；缺失：优先股账面价值、总股息原口径 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：不支持原定义（缺上述输入）；已有字段只能形成另行命名的简化/适配版本 |

| CRSPM.divi；PF02245 | div = cash_div.fillna(0)；divinit = (div &gt; 0) &amp; (rolling(div, 24, 'sum', lag=1) == 0)；char = rolling(divinit, 6, 'sum') &gt; 0；输出=char.astype(config.float_type)；[确切算法与预处理](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L2429)；全部来源：[PyAnomaly · CRSPM.divi](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L2429) | `ann_date`（`financial_dividend`）；`imp_ann_date`（`financial_dividend`）；`ex_date`（`financial_dividend`）；`cash_div`（`financial_dividend`）；`div_proc`（`financial_dividend`）；`close_hfq`（`stock_day`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原数学定义、原始科目映射、原样本过滤或完整计算/缺失回退规则尚未逐项核定；暂不列为可直接实现因子，不以简化近似冒充原项 |
| CRSPM.divo；PF02246 | div = cash_div.fillna(0)；div3 = lag(div, 3)；div6 = lag(div, 6)；div9 = lag(div, 9)；div12 = lag(div, 12)；div15 = lag(div, 15)；div18 = lag(div, 18)；div24 = lag(div, 24)；char1 = (div == 0) &amp; (div3 &gt; 0) &amp; (div6 &gt; 0) &amp; (div9 &gt; 0) &amp; (div12 &gt; 0) &amp; (div15 &gt; 0) &amp; (div18 &gt; 0)；char2 = (div == 0) &amp; (div3 == 0) &amp; (div6 &gt; 0) &amp; (div9 == 0) &amp; (div12 &gt; 0) &amp; (div15 == 0) &amp; (div18 &gt; 0)；char3 = (div == 0) &amp; (div3 == 0) &amp; (div6 == 0) &amp; (div9 == 0) &amp; (div12 &gt; 0) &amp; (div15 == 0) &amp; (div18 == 0) &amp; (div24 &gt; 0)；divomit = char1 &#124; char2 &#124; char3；char = rolling(divomit, 2, 'sum') &gt; 0；输出=char.astype(config.float_type)；[确切算法与预处理](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L2445)；全部来源：[PyAnomaly · CRSPM.divo](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L2445) | `ann_date`（`financial_dividend`）；`imp_ann_date`（`financial_dividend`）；`ex_date`（`financial_dividend`）；`cash_div`（`financial_dividend`）；`div_proc`（`financial_dividend`）；`close_hfq`（`stock_day`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原数学定义、原始科目映射、原样本过滤或完整计算/缺失回退规则尚未逐项核定；暂不列为可直接实现因子，不以简化近似冒充原项 |

| CRSPD.zero_trades_21d；PF02262 | cd['_tmp'] = cd['vol'] == 0；gb = get_idym_group()；cnt = gb.turnover_d.count()；zero_days = gb._tmp.sum()；turnover = gb.turnover_d.sum()；zero_days = (zero_days + where(turnover == 0, 0, 1 / turnover / 480000)) * 21 / cnt；zero_days[cnt == 0] = nan；输出=zero_days.to_numpy()；[确切算法与预处理](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3266)；全部来源：[PyAnomaly · CRSPD.zero_trades_21d](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3266) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；`vol`（`stock_day`）；`amount`（`stock_day`）；`total_share`（`stock_day`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：支持（字段设计；历史完整性未核验）；原美股交易所/样本筛选不自动复制到中证500 |
| CRSPD.bidaskhl_21d；PF02263 | cd = data；bidlo, askhi = _adjust_bidaskhl(prc_adj.to_numpy(), bidlo_adj.to_numpy(), askhi_adj.to_numpy(), get_row_count())；prc_l1 = lag(prc.values)；lo_l1 = lag(bidlo, 1)；hi_l1 = lag(askhi, 1)；cond = (prc_l1 &lt; bidlo) &amp; (prc_l1 &gt; 0)；lo_t = where(cond, prc_l1, bidlo)；hi_t = where(cond, askhi - bidlo + prc_l1, askhi)；cond = (prc_l1 &gt; askhi) &amp; (prc_l1 &gt; 0)；lo_t = where(cond, bidlo + prc_l1 - askhi, lo_t)；hi_t = where(cond, prc_l1, hi_t)；const = 3 - 2 * 2 ^ 0.5；hi_2d = maximum(hi_t, hi_l1)；lo_2d = minimum(lo_t, lo_l1)；alpha = (2 ^ 0.5 - 1) * sqrt(beta) / const - sqrt(gamma / const)；cd['_tmp'] = maximum(2 * (exp(alpha) - 1) / (1 + exp(alpha)), 0)；char = get_idym_group()['_tmp'].mean().to_numpy()；输出=char；[确切算法与预处理](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3312)；全部来源：[PyAnomaly · CRSPD.bidaskhl_21d](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3312) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：支持（字段设计；历史完整性未核验）；原美股交易所/样本筛选不自动复制到中证500 |

| CRSPD.betabab_1260d；PF02278 | corr_1260d := cd = data；data = loc[:, ['mon']].copy()；data['exret_3l'] = rolling(exret, 3, 'mean')；data['mktrf_3l'] = rolling(mktrf, 3, 'mean')；输出=_apply_to_ids(data, 'corr_1260d', 1)；if created('rvol_252d'):<br>    return；icolumns = ['mon', 'exret', 'mktrf', 'prc_adj', 'vol', 'turnover_d']；ocolumns = ['rvol_252d', 'mktvol_252d', 'ivol_capm_252d', 'prc_highprc_252d', 'zero_trades_252d', 'betadown_252d']；data[ocolumns] = _apply_to_ids(icolumns, 'roll_252d', 6)；输出=data.corr_1260d * data.rvol_252d / data.mktvol_252d；[确切算法与预处理](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3459)；全部来源：[PyAnomaly · CRSPD.betabab_1260d](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3459) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`）；`vol`（`stock_day`）；`amount`（`stock_day`）；`total_share`（`stock_day`）；`close`（`zz500_daily_index`）；缺失：市场超额收益；缺失：匹配的市场超额收益及rf；中证500收益只能作为另命名基准版本 | C4 核心定义或经济映射歧义；中至高（补参数不足以保证同因子）；现有字段或必要原始经济科目不足：不支持原定义（缺上述输入）；可另行定义简化版本 |
| CRSPD.beta；PF02279 | if created('pricedelay'):<br>    return；vars = cd[['ret', 'ym']].reset_index()；vars['yw'] = vars.date.dt.isocalendar().year * 100 + vars.date.dt.isocalendar().week；gb = vars.groupby(['permno', 'yw'])；reg = DataFrame(exp(gb.wkret.sum()) - 1)；reg['ym'] = gb.ym.nth(-1).to_numpy()；reg['ewret'] = reg.groupby(['yw']).wkret.transform('mean')；reg['ewret_l1'] = reg['ewret'].shift(1)；reg['ewret_l2'] = reg['ewret'].shift(2)；reg['ewret_l3'] = reg['ewret'].shift(3)；reg['ewret_l4'] = reg['ewret'].shift(4)；gb = reg.groupby('permno')；reg.loc[gb.cumcount() &lt; 4, ['ewret_l1', 'ewret_l2', 'ewret_l3', 'ewret_l4']] = nan；gsize = gb.size().to_numpy()；data = reg[['wkret', 'ewret', 'ewret_l1', 'ewret_l2', 'ewret_l3', 'ewret_l4']].to_numpy()；res = apply_to_groups_jit(data[:, :2], gsize, rolling_regression, 4, 156, 52, True)；reg['beta'] = res[:, 1]；reg['betasq'] = reg['beta'] ^ 2；reg['idiovol'] = res[:, -1]；r2 = res[:, -2]；res = apply_to_groups_jit(data, gsize, rolling_regression, 8, 156, 52, True)；beta2 = res[:, :-2]；r2_multi = res[:, -2]；reg['pricedelay'] = 1 - r2 / r2_multi；reg['pricedelay_slope'] = (beta2[:, 2:] * array([1, 2, 3, 4])).sum(1) / beta2[:, 1:].sum(1)；reg_ym = reg.reset_index().drop_duplicates(subset=['ym', 'permno'], keep='last')；reg_ym.index.names = ('date', 'permno')；ocolumns = ['beta', 'betasq', 'idiovol', 'pricedelay', 'pricedelay_slope']；data[ocolumns] = reg_ym.loc[:, ocolumns]；输出=data['beta']；[确切算法与预处理](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3517)；全部来源：[PyAnomaly · CRSPD.beta](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3517) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：支持（字段设计；历史完整性未核验）；原美股交易所/样本筛选不自动复制到中证500 |

| CRSPD.baspread；PF02284 | cd = data；cd['_tmp'] = (askhi - bidlo) / (0.5 * (askhi + bidlo))；输出=get_idym_group()._tmp.mean().to_numpy()；[确切算法与预处理](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3548)；全部来源：[PyAnomaly · CRSPD.baspread](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3548) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：支持（字段设计；历史完整性未核验）；原美股交易所/样本筛选不自动复制到中证500 |
| CRSPD.trend_factor；PF02285 | ret = cd[['ym', 'ret']].reset_index(level=0, drop=True).set_index('ym', append=True)；ret['idx'] = arange(ret.shape[0])；gb = ret.groupby(level=[0, 1])；ret = exp(gb['ret'].sum()) - 1；idx = gb['idx'].last().to_numpy()；prc_adj = cd['prc_adj'].to_numpy()；avg_prc = full([ret.shape[0], 12], nan)；avg_prc[:, 0] = prc_adj[idx]；avg_prc[:, 1] = rolling(prc_adj, 3, 'mean')[idx]；avg_prc[:, 2] = rolling(prc_adj, 5, 'mean')[idx]；avg_prc[:, 3] = rolling(prc_adj, 10, 'mean')[idx]；avg_prc[:, 4] = rolling(prc_adj, 20, 'mean')[idx]；avg_prc[:, 5] = rolling(prc_adj, 50, 'mean')[idx]；avg_prc[:, 6] = rolling(prc_adj, 100, 'mean')[idx]；avg_prc[:, 7] = rolling(prc_adj, 200, 'mean')[idx]；avg_prc[:, 8] = rolling(prc_adj, 400, 'mean')[idx]；avg_prc[:, 9] = rolling(prc_adj, 600, 'mean')[idx]；avg_prc[:, 10] = rolling(prc_adj, 800, 'mean')[idx]；avg_prc[:, 11] = rolling(prc_adj, 1000, 'mean')[idx]；avg_prc /= avg_prc[:, [0]]；avg_prc = DataFrame(avg_prc, index=ret.index)；sample = concat([ret, avg_prc.groupby(level=0).shift(1)], axis=1)；beta = {}；for ym, sample_ in sample.groupby('ym'):<br>    sample_.dropna(inplace=True)<br>    y = sample_.iloc[:, 0]<br>    X = sample_.iloc[:, 1:]<br>    if X.shape[0] &lt; X.shape[1]:<br>        beta[ym] = full(X.shape[1], nan)<br>    else:<br>        beta[ym] = linalg.solve(X.T @ X, X.T @ y).reshape(X.shape[1])；beta = DataFrame.from_dict(beta, orient='index')；est_beta = beta.rolling(12).mean()；char = []；for ym, avg_prc_ in avg_prc.groupby('ym'):<br>    char.append(avg_prc_.dot(est_beta.loc[ym]))；输出=concat(char).sort_index().to_numpy()；[确切算法与预处理](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3605)；全部来源：[PyAnomaly · CRSPD.trend_factor](https://github.com/chulwoohan/pyanomaly/blob/59758c913a4146bbfb9d3c056668ccdc5231c8c8/pyanomaly/characteristics.py#L3605) | `close_hfq`（`stock_day`） | C6 实现契约尚待验证；中（不是数学不可能）；原公式/输入/时点口径尚未闭合：支持（字段设计；历史完整性未核验）；原美股交易所/样本筛选不自动复制到中证500 |
